\texttt{CELEST.WP5E.WM.01} \mE/\mC\ (executed, the constructive $w_m$ derivation --- verdict
ERFOLG per the frozen preregistration: the $1/\det_j$ fixed-point weight COMPUTED from three
independent sources (the equivariant mode ledger with Abel value $(1/2,1/4,1/2)$; the
zeta/Quillen determinant $4\sin^2(\pi j/4)$ with the unique real positive section; the
$\delta_{1f}$ block constant term $1/\det_b$), the v516 chain reproduced number by number
($w=4h$, $c=4=|\mu_4|$, squares $(18,9,18)$, total $45\langle x,x\rangle^2$, $\psi\pm64$),
all negative controls behaving (the $\Z_2$/EH anchor at $c=2=|\Z_2|$, the $SO(16)$ kill, the
$\operatorname{diag}(i,i)$ break, the $k=3,5$ wandering); typed premises
TP-REG/TP-Q/TP-NUM/TP-CH stay \mC; the global BCOV integral beyond the fibre zero-mode factor
stays \mO);
\texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} untouched, stays \mO.

% =====================================================================
\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01} (the Osterwalder--Schrader twistor bridge)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} \textbf{This is the actual bridge from compiler to physics --- everything in
\texttt{CELEST.SEAM.01} is preparation for it.} It is a research contract \mO\ (ledger row
\texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01}: Open, research contract), \emph{not} a claim; nothing below asserts
that the construction exists. The external programme it engages --- Woit's Euclidean Twistor
Unification (\texttt{arXiv:2104.05099}) --- is a \emph{named reference frame} for the shape of the
target, never a confirmation in either direction (the Woit-bridge paragraph in \texttt{tfpt\_3}
states exactly what is missing).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Input.} $X_+=(\mathbb{PT}/\Gamma)_+$ (the positive-time half of the
quotiented projective twistor space; $\Gamma=\Gamma_{\mathrm{ALE}}\cong\Z_4$, normalised by the
clock --- the definition block of \texttt{CELEST.SEAM.01}); $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$, the
\emph{interacting} open$+$closed twistorial algebra (SDYM$(E_8)$ open strings $+$ BCOV closed
strings --- the WP5e-proper object, whose free/equivariant skeleton is pinned by
\vref{v492}--\vref{v517}); $\rho$, the order-$4$ clock $\operatorname{diag}(i,1)$; $\Theta$, the
anti-linear real structure induced by the seam reflection; and $\mu_{\mathrm{BCOV+SDYM}}$, the
interacting functional (the global BCOV$+$SDYM quantisation on $\mathbb{PT}/\Gamma$).
\emph{Precision (i), from the $\alpha$ stage:} ``induced by the seam reflection'' means the
seam-circle \emph{reflection}, not the deck/covering involution --- the deck (whose free-ness is
topology, \vref{v510}) furnishes the $(-1)^F$ Kramers class $\Theta_t^2=(-1)^F$, not the OS
$\Theta$; the seam-circle reflection furnishes $\Theta_{\mathrm{Fock}}^2=+1$
(\veri{v519\_woit\_theta\_rp\_free.py}).
\emph{Precision (ii):} on the RP side the $\mu_4$ marks sit at the \emph{bond midpoints} of the
$16$-Majorana seam circle (the half-offset/NS-natural placement, $4$ sites per mark quadrant) ---
the cut through the sites fails reflection positivity exactly
(\veri{v519\_woit\_theta\_rp\_free.py}).
\emph{Precision (iii), from the $\beta_1$ stage:} ``gauge-invariant subalgebra'' at the free
level means the GSO/fermion-parity $\Z_2$ --- the only $\theta$-compatible part of the $\mu_4$
tower (the free-fermion shadow of the $E_8$ glue: $(E_8)_1=$ even NS of $SO(16)_1$ $+$ R
spinor); the clock is the euclidean rotation itself (\emph{timelike}: all $16$ seam mirror axes
invert it, none commutes), and its equivariant statement moves to $\beta_2$ --- OS quotient
first, then the clock as reconstructed transfer operator
(\veri{v522\_woit\_beta1\_gso\_gauge.py}).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Target theorem.} $\Theta^2=1$ and $\Theta\rho\Theta=\rho^{-1}$; the
gauge-invariant algebra is reflection-positive; and the Osterwalder--Schrader quotient produces
$(\mathcal H,\Omega,U(\mathcal P^\uparrow_+),\mathcal A_{\mathrm{Mink}})$ with: a positive Hilbert
metric, positive energy, local causality, \emph{one chiral fermion generation without mirror
doubling}, the TFPT charge lattice, an internal $SU(3){\times}SU(2){\times}U(1)$ action, and the
$(E_8)_1$ seam net as an \emph{actual} boundary net (not only as a character).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Kill tests (pre-registered, all seven live).}
\textbf{(1)} $\Theta$ is incompatible with the clock (no anti-linear $\Theta$ with $\Theta^2=1$ and
$\Theta\rho\Theta=\rho^{-1}$ exists on $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$).
\textbf{(2)} Reflection positivity fails after gauge fixing (the gauge-invariant subalgebra of
$\mu_{\mathrm{BCOV+SDYM}}$ is not RP).
\emph{Named toy threat (no status change):} on the first genuinely interacting $16$-Majorana
Fidkowski--Kitaev seam toy (\texttt{SEAM.INT.FKTOY.01}, \veri{v529\_seam\_interacting\_toy\_fk.py})
Kill-Test 1 does \emph{not} fire ($\Theta$ exists with $\Theta^2=+1$ and clock-tower inversion),
but Kill-Test 2 \emph{fires at toy level} --- RP breaks for every $g>0$ by an interference
mechanism, with the \emph{straddle law} (RP fails exactly on quartet-straddled cuts, stays PD on
quartet-avoiding ones; $24/24$) as a concrete hurdle for the interacting stages ($\gamma$ and
beyond; the free/quotient-level $\beta_3$ is since executed, \vref{v565}). Fence: one toy / one
interaction class / \mC\ flat-band parent --- this narrows the route (every $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$
must protect RP against exactly this mechanism), it does not close it.
\emph{The hurdle, executed as a selector (\texttt{SEAM.STRADDLE.CONE.01},
\veri{v534\_seam\_straddle\_cone.py}; no status change):} on the equivariant interacting mark
family (independent couplings on the four mark quartets, both signs, every admissible cut ---
including the $\pi/2$ straddled clock axes the $24$-entry law had not covered) reflection
positivity survives for \emph{exactly one} member $\times$ sign: $\delta=\pi/2$ with positive
coupling, PD on all four cuts over $g\in\{1/32..8\}$ with the minimum eigenvalue lifted
\emph{away} from the RP boundary; every asymmetric member dies. The straddle law refines
(asymmetric straddling kills, symmetric straddling protects), the literal leading-order cone
formalization is dead (the protection is nonperturbative), and the threat paragraph gains its
complement: a passing interaction class \emph{exists} and its unique survivor is the alignment
bit --- the same member that carries the \vref{v528} twist-class order parameter. Toy-level
evidence for a dynamical origin of the bit, not a derivation; the kill tests stay formally live
on $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$.
\emph{The law derived, and the counterweight given its first OS-viable cut geometry
(2026-08-27, \texttt{SEAM.INT.ODDSPLIT.01} $+$ \texttt{SEAM.INT.OSAVOID.01},
\veri{v972\_seam\_interaction\_front.py}; toy-level census, the v529 fence inherited, no
status change):} the straddle law refines to the \emph{odd-split law} --- the
Jaffe--Pedrocchi sufficient condition for Majorana reflection positivity
(\texttt{arXiv:1406.1384}, \texttt{arXiv:1601.01994}), made decidable, \emph{predicts} RP
survival on the centre-straddled ($2{+}2$ mirror-split) cuts and measurement confirms it,
with the predicted-vs-measured RP confusion matrix perfect over all $35$ (member, cut)
cells; the $\mu_4$-covariant quartic space (dimension $464$) contains \emph{no}
JP-certifiable direction on all cuts. And the threat gains a positive counterweight: the
quartet-avoiding cut family of the clock-invariant $m{=}4$ member generates the \emph{full}
even algebra (rank $15$, dimension $32768$) and carries a \emph{positive} OS/GNS structure
at $g{=}1$ (minimum eigenvalue $+4.43\times10^{-5}$) while the straddled control fails
($-6.94\times10^{-2}$), and the $\mu_4$ clock maps the avoiding pair onto itself --- the
kill-test-2 counterweight is \emph{narrowed} (an OS-viable cut geometry exists), not
removed.
\textbf{(3)} The reconstruction produces a vector-like mirror generation (chirality is lost in the
OS quotient).
\textbf{(4)} The Penrose transform reaches only free / self-dual states (the interacting extension
does not exist at the Costello--Li grade).
\textbf{(5)} The reconstructed net has the right character but the wrong OPE / fails net equivalence
(character-level agreement without operator-algebraic agreement).
\textbf{(6)} The internal $SU(2)$ remains a spacetime factor (no genuine internal weak factor
separates from the Euclidean rotation group).
\textbf{(7)} The four $\mu_4$ marks are not incidence-compatibly extendable over spacetime (the
mark data of the seam cannot be propagated through the incidence relation).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The $\alpha$ stage --- executed (\emph{done}; the free/equivariant
milestone \texttt{WOIT.THETA.FREE.01}, \veri{v519\_woit\_theta\_rp\_free.py}).} The real structure
\emph{exists}, and free reflection positivity picks the \emph{same} family. Exactly \emph{two}
families of anti-linear structures on $\mathbb C^2$ normalise the clock: family D
($z\mapsto\mu\bar z$, $|\mu|=1$) satisfies $\Theta\rho\Theta=\rho^{-1}$ \emph{exactly} with
$\Theta^2=+1$ ($-1$ impossible: $M\bar M=\operatorname{diag}(|\mu|^2,1)$ --- Kramers-free), inverts
the deck and reflects the seam circle ($2$ cut points); family A ($z\mapsto\mu/\bar z$) centralises
the clock projectively and \emph{never} inverts it --- it \emph{defines} the euclidean section
(Woit's $\rho_{\mathrm{tw}}$ side: $\rho_{\mathrm{tw}}^2=-1$, no real points, replicated exactly on
$\mathbb C^4$), while family D is the OS conjugation ($\sigma_{\mathrm{std}}$, real points
$\mathbb{RP}^3$). Mark compatibility pins $\mu\in\mu_4$ (a $4$-element torsor, $2$ clock orbits);
the $\Z_8$ spin plane has no phase leaks; in exact $\mathrm{Cl}(16)$,
$\Theta_{\mathrm{Fock}}=U_r\circ K$ has $\Theta_{\mathrm{Fock}}^2=2^7>0$ (normalised $+1$), inverts
the Fock clock tower ($V\mapsto 4096\,V^{-1}$) and normalises the deck, while the deck-induced
candidate has $\Theta_t^2=(-1)^F$ --- the \vref{v510} split/nonsplit dichotomy \emph{is} the
$\Theta^2=+1$ vs $(-1)^F$ dichotomy. Free RP holds on the bond cut with \emph{no} degree truncation
(one-particle Gram $(8,0,0)$ PD, even $\deg\le2$ sector $(29,0,0)$, complete $N=8$ half-sided
algebra PD; twist $\eta=+i$ forced), the site cut and the clock-centralising family fail RP
exactly, and the anti-chiral state flips the odd sector to negative definite (the free shadow of
kill test 3). \textbf{Kill test (1) therefore does \emph{not} fire at the free/equivariant level
--- and stays formally \emph{live} on the interacting algebra $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$}; kill
tests (2)--(7) are untouched. No marker moves: the contract stays \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The $\beta_1$ stage --- executed (\emph{done}, verdict UNDECIDED
per the frozen preregistration; the typed milestone \texttt{WOIT.BETA1.GSO.01},
\veri{v522\_woit\_beta1\_gso\_gauge.py}).} ``The clock is time-like, GSO is the gauge datum.''
The preregistered clock-tower reading of ``gauge'' (the $\mu_4$/$\Gamma_{\mathrm{ALE}}$
equivariance averaged \emph{before} the OS quotient) is structurally the \emph{wrong}
operationalisation: the one-step clock insertion
$T_1=\langle\theta(e_a),\alpha_S(e_b)\rangle$ violates Hermiticity \emph{exactly} (witness
entry $-i/(8\sin(5\pi/16))$ where the conjugate transpose demands $+i/(8\sin(5\pi/16))$; the
pairing is complex-\emph{symmetric}: $745$ matching, $96$ anti-matching, $0$ violations ---
``OS-symmetric'' is strictly weaker than Hermitian, so ``positivity after gauge fixing'' is
not even well-posed for the clock reading). The mechanism is typed exactly: \emph{all} $16$
dihedral reflection axes of the NS seam circle invert the clock lift ($RSR^{-1}=S^{-1}$), none
commutes --- the $\mu_4$ clock \emph{is} Woit's euclidean rotation, and the $\theta$-compatible
(gaugeable) part of its $\Z_8$ Fock tower is exactly the $2$-torsion $\{1,(-1)^F\}$ = the
GSO/fermion-parity $\Z_2$ (Hermiticity census over all powers; $\alpha^4=$ the $2\pi$ rotation
of the seam $=$ fermion parity). Under that corrected typing \textbf{gauge-fixed RP holds
exactly}: the parity-projected bond-cut Gram is $(29,0,0)$ PD at $N=16$ ($\deg\le2$, min
eigenvalue $1.78\times10^{-6}$ at $40$ digits) and $(8,0,0)$ PD on the complete $N=8$ half
algebra (no degree truncation); the site-cut defect \emph{survives} gauge fixing ($(7,9,6)$ ---
the bond placement of precision (ii) is gauge-invariant information); family A stays indefinite
($(17,12,0)$ vs family D's $(29,0,0)$: the $\alpha$-stage triangulation survives); the Ramond
(wrap $+1$) ``true $\Z_4$'' lift fails state invariance (the NS/$\Z_8$ tower is forced); and
the Kramers class stays trivial on the invariant sector ($\Theta_t^2=\mathrm{id}$ on
$\mathcal A^G$, $\Theta$ restricts with $\Theta^2=+1$). \textbf{Kill test (2)'s free shadow
does \emph{not} fire} (no legitimate Hermitian gauge-fixed form has a negative direction), and
kill test (1) stays discharged also gauge-invariantly; both stay formally live on
$\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$. Transparency (documented in the module): the first frozen run
scored $6/13$ --- the clock-reading checks failed exactly as the mechanism predicts --- and
the preregistered deg-$2$ clock-invariant dimension $30$ was a combinatorial error (correct
orbit census $28+4=32$); the verdict follows the frozen logic (Hermiticity failure
$\Rightarrow$ structural-failure branch $\Rightarrow$ UNDECIDED). $\beta_1$ \emph{proper}
(the clock-equivariant statement) is re-routed through $\beta_2$. No marker moves: the
contract stays \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The $\beta_2$ stage --- executed (\emph{done}, verdict SUCCESS
per the frozen preregistration, \mC-typed per contract precision (iii); the milestone
\texttt{WOIT.BETA2.OS.01}, \veri{v524\_woit\_beta2\_os\_quotient.py}).} ``The OS quotient of
the free system made explicit.'' $\mathcal H_{\mathrm{phys}} = \mathcal A_+/\mathcal N$ is
\emph{explicit and nondegenerate} at both levels: at $N = 16$ ($\deg\le2$) the $37{\times}37$
bond-cut OS Gram is exactly Hermitian and parity-block-diagonal with inertia $(37, 0, 0)$ PD
(min eigenvalue $1.7801\times10^{-6}$ at $40$ digits) and null space $\{0\}$,
$\dim 37 = 29 \oplus 8$; at $N = 8$ the \emph{complete} half algebra gives $(16, 0, 0)$ PD
with $\dim 16 = 8 \oplus 8 = 4^2$ --- compact euclidean time reconstructs a \emph{thermal}
(KMS) representation, with the exact certificate
$\sin^2(3\pi/8) - \sin(\pi/8)\sin(5\pi/8) = 1/2$. The euclidean rotation becomes a
Klein--Landau \emph{local symmetric semigroup}: $\tau_k = \omega(\theta(a)\,\alpha_k(b))$ is
exactly Hermitian on every shrinking domain $D_k$ ($k = 1..7$, dims $29, 22, 16, 11, 7, 4,
2$), the chain identities are exact and the vacuum is fixed; the positivity pattern \emph{is}
the $\alpha$-stage site/bond dichotomy (even steps PSD via the exact square identity
$T(2j) = A_j^*A_j$, odd steps indefinite with exactly zero one-particle diagonal --- the
one-step transfer is \emph{not} positive: the free chirality datum, kill-test-3 shadow
sharpened). \textbf{The clock is implemented on $\mathcal H_{\mathrm{phys}}$ as the positive
self-adjoint transfer step $T^{N/4}$} ($(11,0,0)$ PD, min $6.7\times10^{-5}$; compressed
spectrum $[0.0195, 1.6414]$ with vacuum eigenvalue exactly $1$; spectral projections
certified at $\sim10^{-40}$ --- exactly the calculus the non-Hermitian pre-quotient average
of \vref{v522} could not have), and at $N = 8$ the compressed clock spectrum is
\emph{exactly} $\{1, \sqrt2 - 1\} = \{1, 1/\delta_S\}$ in both parity sectors (the silver
axes of the \vref{v519} $\mu_4$ torsor returning as the clock eigenvalue); the reconstructed
rotation group $U(s) = e^{isH}$ is unitary with the group law --- per precision (iii) the
\mC-operationalisation of the contract phrase ``the clock acting unitarily''. The
\vref{v522} non-Hermiticity is \emph{resolved}: the census $(745, 96, 0)$ is reproduced,
every anti-matching entry is a wrap overlap and every overlap-free entry matches --- the
pre-quotient failure was exactly the domain/wrap artifact. The pre-declared KMS deviation
arrives with exactly the declared witnesses: \emph{no contraction} on the compact circle
(edge-mode expansion $C(1)/C(3) = 1 + \sqrt2 = \delta_S$ exactly; $\det(G - \tau_4) < 0$) ---
a typed KMS finding, not a $\beta_2$ failure (the contract demands no contraction).
GSO/$\Theta$: the grading survives; $\theta_{\mathrm{cut}}$ and the deck candidate do
\emph{not} act on $\mathcal H_{\mathrm{phys}}$ (support censuses); the \emph{perpendicular}
torsor mirror descends anti-unitarily ($\eta_\perp \in \{\pm i\}$) with
$\Theta_{\mathrm{phys}}^2 = +1$ on \emph{every} sector --- the quotient is Kramers-free ---
and $\theta_{\mathrm{cut}}\circ\theta_\perp = \alpha_{N/2}$ exactly (the torsor closes onto
the deck). Controls: the site cut is indefinite ($(3,3,1)/(2,2,4)$ --- the contract kill
branch fires for the site placement), family A admits no quotient ($(4,4,0)$), the
anti-chiral state flips to $(8,8,0)$ (quotient existence itself selects the chiral
orientation), the wrong semigroup direction is non-Hermitian exactly off-domain (all
mismatches wrap-overlap). \textbf{Kill tests (1) and (2) are strengthened and the (3) shadow
is sharpened; none fires} --- all seven stay formally live on
$\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$. Transparency (documented in the module): run 1 scored $18/20$
--- a sympy simplify failure on a \emph{true} $\pi/16$ product formula
($\sin(7\pi/16) - \sin(5\pi/16) = 2\cos(3\pi/8)\sin(\pi/16)$), fixed by a Laurent-polynomial
certificate; no criterion, tolerance or expectation changed. No marker moves: the contract
stays \mO; $\beta_3$ is executed next (below).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The $\beta_3$ stage, executed
(\texttt{WOIT.BETA3.DUALITY.01}, \veri{v565\_woit\_beta3\_pt\_duality.py}; verdict SUCCESS per
the frozen preregistration; no status change).}  The $\mathbb{PT}\leftrightarrow\mathbb{PT}^*$
duality is \emph{constructed as a compatibility class} and typed against
$\sigma_{\mathrm{std}}$, with the contract kill branch decided \emph{empty by algebra}.
\textbf{(i) The class:} a duality is a nondegenerate Hermitian form $\Phi$ on $\mathbb{C}^4$
($\sigma(Z)=Z^\dagger\Phi$, mapping $\mathbb{PT}\to\mathbb{PT}^*$); clock compatibility has the
entrywise residual $(\bar\lambda_j\lambda_k-1)\Phi_{jk}$ and forces the four cross-eigenspace
entries to zero exactly: two $2\times2$ Hermitian blocks, $16\to8$ real parameters (the deck
adds nothing; the wrong clock lift $\operatorname{diag}(i,i,1,1)$ breaks the split).
\textbf{(ii) The dichotomy and the forced $(2,2)$:} pairing against Woit's Euclidean structure
$\rho_{\mathrm{tw}}$ via $M_{\mathrm{tw}}^\dagger\Phi M_{\mathrm{tw}}=\varepsilon\bar\Phi$
splits the class exactly in two.  The \emph{Euclidean} branch ($\varepsilon=+1$;
$\sigma_{\mathrm{std}}=\mathbf 1$ lives here) has seam-line norm $a(|z|^2+1)$: no null point,
no OS cut, ever --- exactly the section-defining role Woit assigns it.  The \emph{Minkowski}
branch ($\varepsilon=-1$) has seam-line norm $a(|z|^2-1)$: \textbf{the null locus meets the
seam line exactly in the seam circle} --- the OS cut is \emph{forced}, not imposed --- and the
second block is exactly minus the conjugate of the first (charpoly mirrored), so the
\textbf{total signature is $(2,2)$ for every nondegenerate member}: Woit's Minkowski signature
is a theorem of clock $+$ $\rho_{\mathrm{tw}}$ pairing, not an input.  \textbf{(iii) The kill
is empty:} every compatible duality inverts clock \emph{and} deck exactly (family D); a
clock-centralising (family A) duality would need $\rho_4^2=$ scalar, but
$\rho_4^2=\operatorname{diag}(-1,1,-1,1)$ --- no solution; and family A independently has no
quotient ($(4,4,0)$, the v524 control).  \textbf{(iv) The identification:} every
seam-preserving Minkowski member induces \emph{one} projective conjugation
$z\to-\bar z$ ($\mu=-1$, fixed points $\pm i$), perpendicular to $\sigma_{\mathrm{std}}$'s
$z\to+\bar z$; under the declared RP-side placement (v519 precision (ii); exact
rational-$\pi$ arithmetic, offsets $\mp\pi/2$) that member \textbf{is} $\theta_\perp$ --- the
mirror whose quotient descent \vref{v524} certified: $\Theta_{\mathrm{phys}}$ is the shadow of
the $\mathbb{PT}\leftrightarrow\mathbb{PT}^*$ duality, anti-unitary, Kramers-free
($\Theta^2=+1$ per sector), time-reversing the semigroup, closing with the
$\sigma_{\mathrm{std}}$-type cut onto the deck ($\theta_{\mathrm{cut}}\circ\theta_\perp
=\alpha_{N/2}$; upstairs: the clock conjugation exchanges $\mu=+1\leftrightarrow\mu=-1$).
\emph{Role separation complete:} $\sigma_{\mathrm{std}}\sim$ the OS cut (the metric), the
$\Phi$-twisted Minkowski duality $\sim$ the descending mirror.  Free/quotient level only;
$\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$ untouched; all seven kill tests stay live there; the declared
conventions (seam line, seam preservation, placement, torsor relabeling) are cited, not
invented.  No marker moves: the contract stays \mO; $\gamma$ is the next milestone.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Thermal follow-on (no status change).} The free OS quotient of
$\beta_2$ is KMS, and its temperature normalisation closes onto the Nariai/BH chain as the
\emph{third leg} of $c_3$ (\texttt{SEAM.THERMAL.KMS.01},
\veri{v526\_seam\_thermal\_kms\_nariai\_bridge.py}: $\beta$ measured $=N$ at both levels;
$\beta_{\mathrm{angle}}=2\pi=1/(4c_3)$, $T_{\mathrm{seam}}=4c_3$; modular Hamiltonian closed;
class-$1$ anti-numerology selection; non-compact $\Rightarrow T=0$). The silver clock
eigenvalue is explained and demystified in parallel (\texttt{SEAM.CLOCK.SILVER.01},
\veri{v527\_seam\_clock\_silver\_spectrum.py}: structure SUCCESS, connection KILL). No marker
moves.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The $\beta$/$\gamma$ roadmap (named milestones, each with success
and kill criteria).}
\textbf{($\beta_1$)} $\Theta$ on the gauge-invariant subalgebra $+$ gauge-fixed RP on the
equivariant SDYM$(E_8)$ sector --- success: the v519 OS form extends to the gauge-invariant
subalgebra with positivity after gauge fixing; kill: kill test (2) fires (RP fails after gauge
fixing). \emph{Executed via \vref{v522} (above): UNDECIDED under the frozen preregistration ---
the clock reading is time-like (wrong operationalisation), the gauge datum is the GSO $\Z_2$,
gauge-fixed RP holds under that typing, neither kill fires; the clock-equivariant statement
moves to $\beta_2$.}
\textbf{($\beta_2$)} The OS quotient of the \emph{free} system made explicit --- success:
$(\mathcal H,\Omega)$ constructed from the v519 bond-cut form with the clock acting unitarily;
kill: the quotient degenerates (zero or indefinite completion). \emph{Executed via
\vref{v524} (above): SUCCESS, \mC-typed per precision (iii) --- $\mathcal H_{\mathrm{phys}}$
explicit and PD at both levels, the clock a positive transfer step with spectral calculus
(exact $N = 8$ spectrum $\{1, \sqrt2-1\}$) and a reconstructed unitary rotation group; the
pre-declared KMS deviation carries exactly the silver witnesses; neither kill fires;
$\beta_3$ is next.}
\textbf{($\beta_3$)} The $\mathbb{PT}\leftrightarrow\mathbb{PT}^*$ duality typed against
$\sigma_{\mathrm{std}}$ --- success: Woit's Minkowski conjugation ($\mathbb{PT}\to\mathbb{PT}^*$)
identified with the family-D conjugation on the quotient; kill: the duality forces a
clock-centralising (family A) structure instead. \emph{Executed via \vref{v565} (above):
SUCCESS --- the kill branch is empty by algebra (every clock-compatible duality is family D;
family A would need $\rho_4^2=$ scalar), the OS cut and the $(2,2)$ signature are forced on
the Minkowski branch, and the unique induced seam member is $\theta_\perp$, the v524

\textbf{($\gamma$)} The chirality theorem $+$ the mark incidence --- success: the odd-sector
definiteness flip of v519 upgraded to ``one chiral generation without mirrors'' on the
reconstructed net, and the four $\mu_4$ marks extended incidence-compatibly; kill: kill tests
(3)/(6)/(7) fire. \emph{The FREE slice is executed via
\veri{v608\_gamma\_free\_slice.py} (9/9, GAMMA-FREE-SLICE-LANDED): on the explicit v524 net
the mirror state's odd sector is \emph{strictly negative definite} (sector-exact flip ---
every mirror fermion vector has negative OS norm, so no mirror mode survives reconstruction:

(clock spectrum exactly $\{1,\sqrt2-1\}$), and the mark transport exists ($\tau_2$ is the
mark-A$\to$B transport, Hermitian PD: kill test (7) does not fire at the free level).
$\gamma$ itself stays open on $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$ (kill tests (3)/(6)/(7) formally
live there); kill test (6) is untouched by the free slice.  The INTERACTING toy slice is
since executed via \veri{v615\_gamma\_toy\_interacting.py} (11/11, GAMMA-TOY-LANDED): on
the RP-surviving interaction class (the alignment bit $\delta=\pi/2$, $s=+$, \vref{v534})
the mirror state's odd sector is \emph{strictly negative definite} on every admissible cut
and every coupling $g\in\{1/32..8\}$ (bounded away from zero --- kill test (3) cannot fire
on the surviving class), the mark transport $\alpha_4$ is Hermitian PD at every $g$ (kill
test (7) does not fire at the interacting toy level), the odd transfer spectrum stays
multiplicity-free (honest finding: strong-coupling near-doubling at $g=8$ without
closing), and the RP-dead member has no gamma home --- the dynamical bit selection and the

class; $\gamma$ proper stays open on $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$.  Kill test (6) has since
received its first (kinematic) slice via \veri{v616\_su2\_internal\_kinematic.py} (17/17,
SU2-KINEMATIC-SEPARATED): on $\mathbb C^4=\mathbb H^2$ the internal su(2) (the right
quaternion action, with $\rho_{\mathrm{tw}}=$ right-$j$ verbatim) preserves every twistor
fiber, commutes exactly with the spacetime quaternion action and intersects it in $\{0\}$
--- the internal factor \emph{separates} kinematically, the clock factorizes exactly as

net) stays open and formally live on $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{hol}}$.}

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Scope fence (explicit non-claims of the celestial/twistor branch).}
Self-dual is \emph{not} full 4D physics. Until and unless this contract closes, the celestial/twistor
branch claims \emph{none} of the following: both helicities (the anti-self-dual completion), generic

\texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} and its route split (\texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.MMST.01}/\texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.TWISTOR.01})
are stated in their own rows and are not moved by anything here. Status:
\texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01} \mO\ (research contract; the $\alpha$ stage is executed ---
\texttt{WOIT.THETA.FREE.01} \mE, \vref{v519} --- with kill test (1) discharged at the free level
only; the $\beta_1$ stage is executed --- \texttt{WOIT.BETA1.GSO.01}, \vref{v522}, verdict
UNDECIDED per the frozen preregistration, with kill test (2)'s free shadow not firing, the

4\mathrm D$ uplift: the seam sphere \emph{is} the Hopf base of $S^3$ (stereographic $=z_1/z_2$
exactly), the crossover $X=S^3/\Z_4$ is the lens boundary of the corpus $A_3$ ALE (deck free,

\section*{Firewall Contract --- \texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01} (no $1{+}1$D theorem closes a $3{+}1$D claim)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Firewall Contract: DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A \emph{standing discipline rule}, in force immediately (ledger row
\texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01}); the uplift theorems it names are separate \mO\ contracts.
It formalizes the core diagnosis of the 2026-08-27 external deep review: \emph{a chiral $1{+}1$D
seam net does not imply a local, interacting $3{+}1$D quantum world}.  The corpus's strongest
finite results are boundary/kinematic building blocks --- the review found several surfaces where
boundary theory, continuous relaxation, modular time, Lorentz geometry and physical 4D dynamics
were in danger of being silently equated.  This firewall separates them by rule.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The rule (two clauses).}
\textbf{(F1)} No theorem whose hypotheses live in $1{+}1$D (a chiral net, a VOA, a lattice
collar, the seam circle) may be cited as \emph{closing} a claim about $3{+}1$D physics.
\textbf{(F2)} Every contract closure must state the spacetime dimension of its \emph{hypotheses}
and of its \emph{conclusion} (dimension typing), with the same discipline as the
\mE/\mC/\mO/\mX\ markers.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Instances audited at registration (honesty census).}
(i)~\texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} and both its routes are $1{+}1$D in \emph{and} out: the cited
scaling-limit framework (Osborne--Stottmeister, \texttt{arXiv:2107.13834}) treats $1{+}1$D free
lattice fermions, the OS-reconstruction selector (Adamo--Moriwaki--Tanimoto,
\texttt{arXiv:2407.18222}) treats 2D full VOAs, and lemma L2 of the externalization package
below claims convergence to a \emph{chiral $c{=}8$ CFT} --- nothing more.  \textbf{Even a fully
proved \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} is a rigorous boundary building block, not the 4D theory.}
(ii)~The \texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01} target theorem (Poincar\'e representation, local causality,
one chiral generation, the internal gauge action) \emph{is} the uplift demand; it stays \mO\ with
all seven kill tests live.
(iii)~The kinematic $2\mathrm D\to3\mathrm D\to4\mathrm D$ uplift (\vref{v957}; the discovery
probe \texttt{experiments/tfpt-discovery/ccc\_uplift\_4d\_probe.py} correctly self-labels as
kinematic) supplies a canonical Hopf fibre / lens crossover / 4D cone with an Einstein Lorentzian
shadow --- \emph{none of which} closes this firewall: a metric uplift is not an interacting local
quantum theory.
(iv)~Anomaly inflow: the non-gappable $c_-{=}8$ edge is naturally the boundary of a
higher-dimensional \emph{invertible/topological} bulk (Witten--Yonekura,
\texttt{arXiv:1909.08775}; Hsieh--Tachikawa--Yonekura, \texttt{arXiv:2003.11550}); the inflow
bulk and the \emph{physical} $3{+}1$D spacetime bulk are two different identifications that must
never be silently equated.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The realistic route ordering (registered as the programme
skeleton).}
\textbf{(1)}~keep the seam as an internal/topological boundary module (all current \mE\ results
survive unchanged as such);
\textbf{(2)}~build a genuine 4D Euclidean lattice theory --- TFPT fixes the representations,
charges, coupling relations and boundary conditions; the 4D lattice carries spacetime, gauge
fields, Higgs and fermions;
\textbf{(3)}~prove the 4D OS package on it (\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} below);
\textbf{(4)}~prove chirality and mirror decoupling \emph{in the same 4D model}
(\texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01} below);
\textbf{(5)}~only then apply Bisognano--Wichmann/CHM and entanglement gravity (the direction
discipline of \texttt{GRAV.NONCIRCULAR.01} below);
\textbf{(6)}~then prove bulk--seam equivalence, identifying the seam as the topological/anomalous
boundary condition of the \emph{constructed} 4D bulk (\texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01} below).
The twistor/celestial alternative (reconstruct the bulk from boundary correlators) is riskier:
boundary correlators do not guarantee bulk locality (cf.\ \texttt{arXiv:2602.04223}); it stays a
named parallel route (\texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01}/\texttt{CELEST.SEAM.01}), not the trunk.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Status.} Firewall in force (F1/F2 bind every future closure);
the uplift theorems live in \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01}, \texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} and
\texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01}, all \mO.

% =====================================================================
\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01} (from seam data to a 4D local quantum theory)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01}:
Open, research contract), \emph{not} a claim.  It makes explicit the theorem that was previously
only implicit between the \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} conclusion (a chiral $c{=}8$ boundary object)
and the \texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01} goal statement (a Poincar\'e-covariant, causal, chiral 4D
theory): \textbf{the missing seam$\to$bulk reconstruction is a theorem of its own, and no
existing corpus result closes any part of it.}

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Input.} The certified seam data: the gapped $\mu_4$-equivariant
collar (\vref{v302}), the index-4 Q-system (\vref{v125}/\vref{v154}), the $(E_8)_1$ character
and modular data (\vref{v377}/\vref{v378}), reflection positivity (\vref{v379}), the clock and
mark structure (\vref{v519}--\vref{v524}), and the kinematic uplift dictionary (\vref{v957}).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Target theorem (all seven demands; none is currently closed).}
From seam data alone, construct a 4D family of local algebras
$\{\mathcal A(O)\}_{O\subset\mathbb R^{1,3}}$ (equivalently a family of Schwinger functions) and
prove:
\textbf{(B1)}~4D locality/microlocality (spacelike commutativity; the correct wavefront sets);
\textbf{(B2)}~Poincar\'e covariance with a positive energy spectrum (the spectrum condition);
\textbf{(B3)}~spin--statistics and CPT;
\textbf{(B4)}~\emph{both} helicities (the anti-self-dual completion --- the known hard wall of
the twistor route);
\textbf{(B5)}~cluster decomposition;
\textbf{(B6)}~a nontrivial S-matrix (the theory is interacting, not generalized-free);
\textbf{(B7)}~a \emph{unique} seam$\leftrightarrow$bulk operator dictionary (which boundary
operator is which bulk field, uniquely --- not only at character level).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Kill tests (pre-registered).}
\textbf{(1)}~every 4D extension of the seam state is a generalized free field (B6 fails
structurally); \textbf{(2)}~the inflow bulk of the $c_-{=}8$ edge is \emph{provably} only
invertible/topological, with no local propagating completion carrying both helicities (B1/B4
fail); \textbf{(3)}~the dictionary is non-unique at operator level (character-level agreement
with OPE mismatch --- the same failure mode as kill test (5) of \texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01}).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Honest relations.} \texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01} (twistor OS
quotient) and the route ordering of \texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01} (constructive 4D
lattice first, seam as its boundary condition) are the two candidate proofs; this contract is
route-agnostic and states only the target.  A Hopf-fiber uplift, a 4D cone, or an Einstein
metric on the bridge (\vref{v957}) does \emph{not} close it.  Status:
\texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01} \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Spectator obstruction (2026-09-04, \veri{v1020\_\allowbreak spectator\_\allowbreak sector\_\allowbreak obstruction.py}).}
Demand (B7) as worded---a \emph{unique} seam$\leftrightarrow$bulk operator dictionary from seam data
alone---is unsatisfiable: for any decoupled anomaly-free sector $C$ with trivial boundary,
$\partial(B\otimes C)=\partial(B)\otimes\mathbf 1=\partial(B)$, so $B$ and $B\oplus C$ share every
seam datum.  A finite free-fermion witness (\veri{v1020\_\allowbreak spectator\_\allowbreak sector\_\allowbreak obstruction.py})
exhibits this concretely (Chern, edge modes and seam-restricted covariance identical for $B$ and
$B\oplus C$; the bulk differs).  Uniqueness is re-scoped to
\texttt{BULK.PRIME\_COMPLETION.01} below (prime/seam-generated completions, trivial
relative commutant); the status of \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01} is unchanged~\mO.

% =====================================================================
\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{BULK.PRIME\_COMPLETION.01} (prime/seam-generated completions, no spectator)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: BULK.PRIME_COMPLETION.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row
\texttt{BULK.PRIME\_COMPLETION.01}: Open, research contract), \emph{not} a claim.
Registered 2026-09-04 from the r644 audit harvest; it replaces the ``unique from seam
data alone'' reading of \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01}/B7 by uniqueness \emph{in the
category of seam-generated (prime) completions}.  \textbf{Dimension typing}
(\texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01} F2): the hypothesis is $2{+}1$D free-fermion,
the conclusion is a re-scoped $4$D demand.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Demand.} (1) an explicit family of seam-derived subalgebras
$\mathcal A_i$ (the seam CAR algebra, its time evolution, inclusions/intersections);
(2) generatedness $\mathcal A_{\mathrm{bulk}}=\mathrm{vN}(\bigvee_i\mathcal A_i)$;
(3) \emph{no-spectator}: trivial relative commutant
$(\bigvee_i\mathcal A_i)'\cap\mathcal A_{\mathrm{bulk}}=\mathbb C\cdot\mathbf 1$,
equivalently no decoupled tensor factor with trivial boundary map; (4) uniqueness
\emph{only} within this category; (5) stability of (2)/(3) in the $3{+}1$D limit.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Kill tests (pre-registered).} A seam-generated completion
with nontrivial relative commutant, or two inequivalent prime completions.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Finite witness (\veri{v1020\_\allowbreak spectator\_\allowbreak sector\_\allowbreak obstruction.py}).}
In the $2{+}1$D free-fermion strip, stacking a trivial gapped sector $C$ (Chern $0$,
gap $>1$) onto the seam model $B$ leaves every seam datum unchanged (Chern
$C_B=C_{B\oplus C}=-1$, $C_C=0$; zero modes $2/0/2$ with $B$-support $1$; edge
dispersion coincident to $1.4{\times}10^{-15}$; seam-restricted covariance identical to
$0$; edge-profile overlap $1$) and changes the bulk (dimension $16$ vs $32$,
$E_0(C)=-25.31$, mid-strip covariance rank $4$ vs $8$,
$\|\Gamma_0-\Gamma_\alpha\|_{\mathrm{HS}}$ $0.752536$ vs $0.760202$).  Krylov
generatedness and the commutant test separate $B$ from $B\oplus C$ by exactly $\dim C$
at every momentum; $\Pi_C=0\oplus\mathbf 1$ lies in the commutant (residual $0$); selection
is unique ($B$ selected, $B\oplus C$ rejected).  \emph{Honest:}
$\mathrm{spectator\_dim}(B)=10$ at $p=0$ only (no onsite term at $M=1$), so the
criterion is ``generated almost everywhere in $p$'', not pointwise.  The $2$D
modular-invariant uniqueness of the holomorphic $(E_8)_1$ net is untouched and is
\emph{not} a $4$D no-spectator theorem.  Status: \texttt{BULK.PRIME\_COMPLETION.01} \mO.

% =====================================================================
\section*{Research Contracts --- \texttt{DYN.MARKOV.EMBED.01} / \texttt{DYN.UNITARY.DILATION.01} (the three meanings of ``dynamics'')}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contracts: DYN.MARKOV.EMBED.01 / DYN.UNITARY.DILATION.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} Two contracts that split what the corpus has loosely called ``dynamics''
into its three inequivalent meanings: \textbf{(a)}~the positive relaxation $T^n$ to a fixed
point; \textbf{(b)}~a continuous dissipative semigroup $e^{t\log T}$; \textbf{(c)}~reversible
Lorentzian/unitary evolution $e^{-itH}$.  Step (a)$\to$(b) is now a machine-checked theorem for
the concrete seam kernel (\mE\ at the finite level); step (b)$\to$(c) is genuinely open and gets
its own contract (its \emph{discrete} collision/QCA leg is meanwhile executed exactly,
\veri{v984\_markov\_qca\_dilation.py}; the continuous legs remain the contract).  Nothing here
moves \texttt{CONTRACT.F.01} or the four observables.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{\texttt{DYN.MARKOV.EMBED.01} --- the finite kernel half is
executed \mE.}  For the v221 seam transfer
kernel $T=J+(2/3)^6\,u_2u_2^{\mathsf T}+(1/3)^6\,u_3u_3^{\mathsf T}$ (spectrum exactly
$\{1,64/729,1/729\}$), the principal logarithm $Q=\log T$ is an \emph{exact classical Markov
generator} \veri{v971\_markov\_embedding\_generator.py}: sympy-exact spectral calculus gives
$e^Q=T$; every row of $Q$ sums to zero
\emph{symbolically}; the three independent off-diagonal entries are exactly
$\{\ln(9/8),\,2\ln 3,\,2\ln 3\}=\{0.117783,\,2.197225,\,2.197225\}$, all positive (the reviewer's
independently verified numbers), with diagonals $-\ln(81/8),-\ln(81/8),-4\ln3$; the
uniformization certificate $Q+(4\ln3)I\ge0$ entrywise (diagonals $\ln8,\ln8,0$, exact) proves
$e^{tQ}=e^{-4t\ln3}\,e^{t(Q+4\ln3\,I)}$ entrywise nonnegative and doubly stochastic for
\emph{all} $t\ge0$ --- so $T$ is embeddable and every root $T^{1/n}=e^{Q/n}$ is again a
stochastic matrix.  The generator spectrum is
$\operatorname{spec}(Q)=\{0,-6\ln(3/2),-6\ln3\}=-\operatorname{spec}(H_{\mathrm{mod}})$
(\vref{v957}: $T=e^{-H_{\mathrm{mod}}}$), the gap is the seam gap $\Delta=6\ln(3/2)$, and $Q$ is
symmetric (detailed balance w.r.t.\ the uniform state --- the KMS/OS-positive free level).
\emph{Negative control (embeddability is not generic):} the same construction with second
eigenvalue $-1/10$ is still doubly stochastic but has $\det=-1/200<0$, while every embeddable
stochastic matrix has $\det e^Q=e^{\operatorname{tr}Q}>0$ --- provably \emph{not} embeddable.
The RP/OS spectral positivity of the seam kernel (\texttt{CONTRACT.F.01} axiom 3) is exactly
what makes (a)$\to$(b) real.  \emph{What stays \mO\ in this contract:} the same embedding
statement at \emph{field} level (on the reconstructed net / quasi-local algebra, uniformly in
system size), not just on the 3-state kernel.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{\texttt{DYN.UNITARY.DILATION.01} --- the (b)$\to$(c) step,
\mO.}  The semigroup $e^{tQ}$ is \emph{irreversible statistical} dynamics --- an H-theorem, not
a Schr\"odinger equation.  Fundamental quantum dynamics requires the chain
$T=e^{-aH}\;\xrightarrow{\ \mathrm{OS}\ }\;U(t)=e^{-itH}$ with $H$ positive self-adjoint, and
the dilation must be \emph{physical}: \textbf{(D1)}~a Stinespring/Hamiltonian dilation of the
semigroup whose generator is (quasi-)local; \textbf{(D2)}~size consistency (the dilation is
compatible with the thermodynamic limit --- no environment that grows pathologically with the
system); \textbf{(D3)}~a Lieb--Robinson bound (finite propagation speed for the dilated
dynamics); \textbf{(D4)}~the OS analytic continuation to a unitary one-parameter group on the
reconstructed Hilbert space.  \emph{Kill tests:} every dilation of the seam semigroup is
provably nonlocal; the Lieb--Robinson velocity diverges in the scaling limit; the OS
continuation fails on the interacting algebra.  \emph{Relation:} demand D2 of
\texttt{CCC.SEAM.CROSSOVER.01} (the Stinespring isometry at field level) already presupposes
this contract; it is hereby named as the prerequisite.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed sharpening --- the lift structure of the finite kernel
(\mE\ at matrix level; the contract itself stays \mO).}  Two modules now pin down \emph{which}
dilations are even candidates.  \textbf{(i)}~\emph{The no-go}: the Kraus dilation the corpus
actually deploys, $K_{ij}=\sqrt{T_{ij}}\,|i\rangle\langle j|$, is measure-and-prepare --- one
step kills every coherence (fully symbolic), its Choi matrix is diagonal in the product basis,
hence the channel is \emph{entanglement breaking} with quantum capacity $0$, and $T>0$
entrywise gives zero-error capacity $C_0(T)=0$ exactly
(\veri{v976\_seam\_lift\_birkhoff.py}); any dilation satisfying D1--D4 must therefore be a
genuinely different object.  \textbf{(ii)}~\emph{The hidden fibre}: the complete set of
random-permutation (Birkhoff) quantizations of the single step $B$ is a one-parameter line
$t\in[0,1/18]$ whose coordinate is the sign-character Fourier coefficient
($\widehat w_t(\mathrm{sgn})=6t$); populations are exactly $t$-free, the one-step coherence
spectrum is $\{1,\,6t,\,(2/3)^{\times2},\,(1/3)^{\times2}\}$, and the triangle loop current is
an exact eigen-observable $\Psi_t(A)=6t\,A$ --- one step and one current readout measure $t$
without an ancilla.  On the contrast qubit the Choi partial transpose has spectrum
$\{-3t/2,\,1/6+3t/2,\,1/3+3t/2,\,1/2-3t/2\}$: the deployed sixth-root spectrum sits
\emph{exactly} on the entanglement-breaking boundary because $2/3+1/3=1$ (mutated spectra miss
by $\mp1/12$), while at the full three-level the family stays NPT at every tested iteration,
$\lambda_{\min}=-(1/3)(2/3)^n$, $t$-independent (\veri{v976\_seam\_lift\_birkhoff.py}).
\textbf{(iii)}~\emph{The coherent end}: $B$ is \emph{unistochastic} --- an exact $U\in SU(3)$
with $|U_{ij}|^2=B_{ij}$ entrywise, unique up to rephasing and complex conjugation, with
Jarlskog invariant $J=\pm1/27$ exactly; no \emph{fixed} microstep unitary generates the
relaxing sequence ($B^2\neq|U^2|^2$; a Dirichlet recurrence witness returns $U^{n^*}$
arbitrarily close to the identity while $B^{n^*}$ is uniform), so repeated steps
\emph{require} environment contact between steps --- precisely the D1--D4 demand --- while the
six-step macro-gate exists as its own reversible $U_6\neq U_1^6$
(\veri{v977\_transfer\_unistochastic\_wilson.py}).  The classical kernel thus admits the full
lift trichotomy \{EB dilation $\mid$ Birkhoff fibre with hidden circulation $t$ $\mid$
reversible macro-gate with hidden orientation $\operatorname{sgn}J$\}: the same classical
data, three inequivalent quantum dynamics.  Ledger rows:
\texttt{TRANSFER.HIDDEN.CIRCULATION.01} and \texttt{TRANSFER.COHERENT.WILSON.01} (executed
halves \mE; the fibre-point/branch selection, the defect--Birkhoff forcing functor --- named
\texttt{TRANSFER.DEFECT.BIRKHOFF.01} --- and the center--transport intertwiner stay \mO).
These results fix the contract's boundary conditions; they do not close it.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, external master-object review wave
2): the \emph{discrete} leg of D1--D3 is realized constructively --- the exact collision-model
dilation (\veri{v984\_markov\_qca\_dilation.py}; the continuous legs stay \mO).}  One fresh
ancilla qutrit per step, the mod-3 record $C|j,k\rangle=|j,j{+}k\rangle$, and the step unitary
$V=C\,(U_B\otimes I)$ (with $U_B$ the \vref{v977} lift) give, as \emph{exact symbolic operator
identities}: $V^\dagger V=I$; the Stinespring identity
$\Phi(\rho)=\operatorname{Tr}_A[V(\rho\otimes|0\rangle\langle0|)V^\dagger]
=\sum_jP_jU_B\rho U_B^\dagger P_j$ (Kraus completeness $\sum_jK_j^\dagger K_j=I$);
$\operatorname{diag}\Phi(\operatorname{diag}p)=Bp$ with vanishing off-diagonals; and six fresh
ancillas give $\operatorname{diag}\Phi^6=B^6p=Tp$ --- the \vref{v977} macro-gate as the reduced

The must-fail fires (the closed unitary has $|U_B^2|^2\neq B^2$), and a typed remark

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} (the genuine 4D Euclidean lattice package)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: QFT4D.OS.RECON.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row \texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01}: Open,
research contract), \emph{not} a claim.  This is step (3) of the
\texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01} route ordering: the \emph{nonperturbative} 4D existence
statement that no perturbative or seam-level result can substitute for.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Input.}  TFPT fixes the discrete data: the representation content
and charge lattice (carrier $+$ glue), the coupling relations at the matching scale, and the
boundary conditions (the seam as internal/topological boundary module).  The 4D Euclidean
lattice carries everything else: spacetime, gauge fields, Higgs, fermions.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Target theorem (the OS package).}  For the so-specified 4D
lattice theory prove: \textbf{(O1)}~reflection positivity of the lattice measure;
\textbf{(O2)}~Euclidean covariance restoration (hypercubic $\to$ full Euclidean group in the
limit); \textbf{(O3)}~clustering; \textbf{(O4)}~uniform bounds (moment/regularity estimates
uniform in lattice spacing and volume); \textbf{(O5)}~existence of the continuum limit;
\textbf{(O6)}~Osterwalder--Schrader reconstruction of the Wightman/Haag--Kastler theory.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Firewall against the perturbative leg (binding phrasing rule).}
The existing all-orders result (\texttt{QFT4D.EG.ALLORDER.01}, \vref{v381}) is a \emph{local
perturbative construction under the stated Epstein--Glaser/BRST hypotheses} --- that and no
more.  The weak adiabatic limit for massless particles exists as a cited theorem (Duch,
\texttt{arXiv:1801.10147}), but its hypotheses must be \emph{verified for the concrete TFPT
model}; a seam-transfer gap does not substitute for the infrared structure of massless
photons/gluons.  Even a fully successful EG/BRST proof yields \emph{none} of: confinement or the
hadron spectrum, the Yang--Mills mass gap, asymptotic charged states in QED (the infraparticle
problem), the nonperturbative Higgs vacuum, or a controlled full S-matrix.  Every corpus phrase
of the form ``the perturbative 4D leg closes to all orders'' is bound to this reading.
Status: \texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Registered decision (2026-08-28,
\texttt{QFT4D.LATTICE.FUNDAMENTAL.01}): the lattice-fundamental reading --- a typed
route/typing, \emph{not} an \mE\ claim and \emph{not} a marker move.}
TFPT's \emph{physical} completeness criterion is the finite, local, unitary lattice quantum
theory (Hamiltonian route: Gauss-law Hilbert space, hermitian local $H$, $T=e^{-aH}$ positive
by construction, Lieb--Robinson cone).  This contract --- the exact continuum
Osterwalder--Schrader limit --- is retyped as \emph{mathematical reinforcement}: a named
strengthening programme, no longer the physical bottleneck.  Poincar\'e invariance is an IR
fixed-point property with experimentally bounded irrelevant Lorentz-violating operators.
Justification anchors (\veri{v989\_\allowbreak lattice\_\allowbreak gate\_\allowbreak battery.py}):
the Euclidean overlap shortcut is killed at T2 (exact; $N_t$-exact confirmation: the
$|\det D_{\rm ov}|^2$ Gram is PSD at $N_t=2$ and indefinite at $N_t=4$, det-only
$\lambda_{\min}\approx-0.249$; Wilson control PSD everywhere --- pinned on overlap time
nonlocality), and the Hamiltonian route clears T2 by construction.  Display: this row stays
\mO\ as the math programme; nothing closes.

is accepted --- ``a single finite box is not a world''.}
The fundamental object is the \emph{quasilocal consistent family} $\{H_\Lambda\}$ with the
thermodynamic-limit dynamics
$\tau_t=\lim e^{itH_\Lambda}A e^{-itH_\Lambda}$.
Binding phrasing: continuum ($a\to0$) \emph{optional} (mathematical reinforcement);
thermodynamic limit ($|\Lambda|\to\infty$) \emph{and} controlled IR universality
\emph{mandatory}.  Decision typing UNCHANGED; no \mE\ claim, no marker move.

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01} (the chiral 4D lattice matter theorem)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row \texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01}:
Open, research contract), \emph{not} a claim.  Step (4) of the firewall route ordering: chirality
and mirror decoupling must be proven \emph{in the same 4D model} that
\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} constructs --- the seam's $c_-{=}8$ and Chern/inflow structure are a
boundary-\emph{anomaly} signal, not yet 4D matter.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Target theorem.}  An explicit chiral lattice operator
(Ginsparg--Wilson/overlap type) for the TFPT representation content with proofs of:
\textbf{(C1)}~a local, gauge-invariant fermion measure; \textbf{(C2)}~perturbative \emph{and}
global anomaly freedom; \textbf{(C3)}~no Witten $SU(2)$ anomaly; \textbf{(C4)}~no mirror
generation in the physical spectrum (the mirror decouples with a mass that survives the
continuum limit); \textbf{(C5)}~the correct index and charges (the TFPT charge lattice,
generation count $\Nfam$); \textbf{(C6)}~a stable continuum limit compatible with the
\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} package.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Calibration (state of the art, honestly).}  The strongest
comparable theorem is Mastropietro's construction (\texttt{arXiv:2303.02790}): a \emph{weakly
coupled} 4D $U(1)$ chiral gauge theory with a \emph{massive} gauge field.  The TFPT target ---
chiral $SU(3){\times}SU(2){\times}U(1)$ with three generations, massless nonabelian gauge
fields, EWSB and confinement downstream --- is far stronger; the gap between the two is the
honest measure of this contract's difficulty.  \emph{Kill tests:} the mirror fails to decouple
(kill test (3) of \texttt{WOIT.OS.TWISTOR.01} at lattice level); a Witten anomaly obstructs the
carrier $SU(2)$; every gauge-invariant measure for the TFPT content is provably nonlocal.
\emph{Consistency note (ledger wins):} the celestial-branch scope fence above honestly lists
interacting RP, mirror decoupling, the internal $SU(2)$, both helicities, local matter fields,
EWSB and confinement as \emph{not} closed; the contradictory ``zero open internal mechanisms''
phrase in \texttt{docs/OPEN\_PROBLEMS.md} is corrected in the same change that registers this
contract (that phrase was a certification statement about a \emph{different}, narrower residual
matrix and had drifted into an overclaim).  Status: \texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01} \mO.

Gauss census is executed on the Hamiltonian route
(\veri{v989\_\allowbreak lattice\_\allowbreak gate\_\allowbreak battery.py}; the $3{+}1$D
contract stays \mO).}

a Gauss statement); $V^4=I$; the mutant $(g_L,g_R)=(1,0)$ on vectorlike content fails.
Honest: not the TFPT representation content and not $3{+}1$D.  Display marker stays \mO.

\section*{Firewall Contract --- \texttt{GRAV.NONCIRCULAR.01} (the Bisognano--Wichmann direction discipline)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Firewall Contract: GRAV.NONCIRCULAR.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A \emph{standing discipline rule}, in force immediately (ledger row
\texttt{GRAV.NONCIRCULAR.01}), with an audit census at registration.  Bisognano--Wichmann (BW)
is a theorem \emph{within} an already-local, Poincar\'e-covariant QFT with a suitable vacuum:
the modular flow of a wedge algebra acts as the boost.  It is \emph{not} a device that generates
Lorentz spacetime from an arbitrary modular flow --- there exist Poincar\'e-covariant nets
violating BW (Mund, \texttt{arXiv:hep-th/0101227}, characterizes when BW holds; Morinelli,
\texttt{arXiv:1703.06831}, exhibits the failure landscape).  The only correct proof order is:
4D net $+$ locality $+$ vacuum $+$ spectrum condition $\Rightarrow$ BW $\Rightarrow$ geometric
modular time.  \emph{Never}: KMS/modular spectrum $\Rightarrow$ Lorentz space $\Rightarrow$ BW.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The rule.}  Every use of BW or of the CHM ball modular
Hamiltonian in the corpus must \emph{declare} whether 4D Poincar\'e covariance is an
\textsc{assumption} (imported; the result is then conditional on
\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01}-class existence) or a \textsc{result} (derived intrinsically).  The
cycle risk this fences: BW/CHM entering the Einstein derivation as input while the 4D
locality/Lorentz structure is still supposed to \emph{come out} of the seam.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Audit census at registration (all current BW/CHM usages,
annotated).}
\begin{itemize}
\item \textsc{assumption} --- the entanglement-equilibrium Einstein derivation
  (\vref{v323}/\vref{v358}/\vref{v359}; \texttt{tfpt\_4\_frontier} and the $(G_{\mathrm{metric}})$
  section above): the CHM ball modular Hamiltonian and the BW boost are \emph{inputs}; the
  derivation is conditional on a 4D Poincar\'e-covariant QFT with the right vacuum existing
  (\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01}) and must never be cited as evidence \emph{for} that existence.
\item \textsc{assumption (declared)} --- \texttt{QG.AMB.01} as a \mC\ redundancy (\vref{v369})
  is everywhere stated ``conditional on \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01}$+$Bisognano--Wichmann''; correct
  as declared.
\item \textsc{assumption (dimension-typed)} --- the seam KMS normalisation chain
  (\vref{v259}/\vref{v526}; $\beta_{\mathrm{angle}}=2\pi$): uses only the universal modular/KMS
  angle of Tomita--Takesaki theory on the \emph{seam} ($1{+}1$D/2D objects), not 4D BW; the
  $2\pi$ is a normalisation, not a spacetime derivation.
\item \textsc{result-direction (open)} --- the intrinsic-BW programme
  (\vref{v438}/\vref{v440}/\vref{v446} and the half-sided modular inclusion route in the
  $(G_{\mathrm{metric}})$ section): the honest attempt to \emph{derive} geometric modular action
  intrinsically; explicitly not closed, and the corpus already records the circularity warning
  (``BW would presuppose the very covariance it should produce'', \vref{v215}).
\item \textsc{evidence, not derivation} --- the compressed-state flow to the CHM/BW geometric
  form (\vref{v478}): a numerical convergence statement in the result direction; it does not
  establish 4D covariance.
\item \textsc{conditional (declared)} --- \texttt{origin\_theory}'s ``BW downstream of the seam
  being a chiral net'': stated as conditional on net identification; correct as declared.
\end{itemize}
Status: firewall in force; no marker moves --- the census confirms the declarations exist but
binds all \emph{future} uses to carry them explicitly.

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{OBS.TRANSDUCTION.01} (the transduction principle: eigenvalue, projection, coupling)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: OBS.TRANSDUCTION.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract (ledger row \texttt{OBS.TRANSDUCTION.01}); the
principle is machine-checked on the minimal model at exploration level, the per-bundle
transduction proofs are \mO.  It elevates the S15 typing rule to a named principle:
\[
  \text{observable signature}
  \;=\;
  \underbrace{\text{internal eigenvalue}}_{\text{compiler \mE}}
  \;+\;
  \underbrace{B\,P_r\neq0}_{\text{transduction}}
  \;+\;
  \underbrace{\text{physical coupling operator}}_{\text{physics}} .
\]
\textbf{Without the middle arrow, neither a non-observation falsifies nor an observation
confirms.}  The minimal model is machine-checked in
\texttt{experiments/\allowbreak theory-contracts/\allowbreak fo01\_transduction\_invisibility.py}: a uniform measurement
functional sees the seam kernel \emph{exactly not at all} ($\mathbf1^{\mathsf T}(T^np-\pi)=0$ to
$10^{-14}$; burst-train statistics KS-indistinguishable from a scrambled control), while a
character-resolved channel reconstructs the eigenvalue $64/729$ to $10^{-10}$ --- invisibility
is a property of the readout $B$, not of the channel.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Demands.}  For each empirical bundle (FRB/GW/pulsar nulls; the
axion/birefringence family; any future search), \emph{prove or refute} $B\,P_r\neq0$ for the
declared readout before the null or the hit is scored.  \emph{Binding application (the
birefringence caution):} the ACT$+$Planck isotropic birefringence being close to
$\beta_{\mathrm{TFPT}}=\varphi_0/(4\pi)$ (\texttt{arXiv:2608.06480}) is an interesting
coincidence, \emph{not} proof, until the shared normalized axion action and the transduction
chain are proven --- the axion bundle ($\beta$, $f_a$, $m_a$, $g_{a\gamma\gamma}$, $\Omega_a$,
EDM) must follow from \emph{one} action with \emph{one} proven coupling.  Status:
\texttt{OBS.TRANSDUCTION.01} --- principle registered, minimal model checked; every per-bundle
middle arrow \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, promote round): a finite $W[J]$
transduction shadow is executed
(\veri{v990\_\allowbreak wj\_\allowbreak transduction\_\allowbreak mechanism.py}; per-bundle
proofs stay \mO).}
On a $1{+}1$D two-site $\mathbb Z_6$ Hamiltonian (species $Q$ and $e^c$), the mixed derivative
$\partial^2 W/\partial J\,\partial\theta$ is nonzero on the seam-coupled link
($\approx-9.3\times10^{-3}$) and \emph{exactly} $0$ on the decoupled $e^c$ control; a
frozen-link companion transduces on the $Q$-staggered operator and stays $0$ on $e^c$.
The middle arrow is thereby a machine-checked finite-level mechanism, not a $4$D bundle proof.
Display: per-bundle empirical proofs stay \mO.

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{PRED.JOINTLIKELIHOOD.01} (from hit-counting to overdetermined relations)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: PRED.JOINTLIKELIHOOD.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row \texttt{PRED.JOINTLIKELIHOOD.01}:
Open, statistics-construction contract), \emph{not} a claim.  The $27{+}$ individually matched
values all descend from the same few atoms ($\varphi_0$, $c_3$, $g_{\mathrm{car}}$, $\mu_4$) and
are therefore statistically \emph{correlated}: counting hits overstates independent evidence.
The evidence is to be reorganized into \emph{overdetermined relation bundles}, each carried by
one proof object:
\textbf{(i)}~the flavor invariant sum rule --- one spurion/operator proof must yield
$\theta_{12}$, $\theta_{13}$, $\delta_{\mathrm{PMNS}}$, $J_{\mathrm{PMNS}}$, the mass splittings
and the CKM texture \emph{together};
\textbf{(ii)}~the inflation consistency relation --- $r=3(1-n_s)^2$ and $\alpha_s=-r/6$
(eliminating $N_\star$ as a fitted middleman);
\textbf{(iii)}~the axion/birefringence bundle --- $\beta$, $f_a$, $m_a$, $g_{a\gamma\gamma}$,
$\Omega_a$ and the EDM bound from the \emph{same} normalized axion action, with the transduction
chain of \texttt{OBS.TRANSDUCTION.01};
\textbf{(iv)}~the character-resolved transfer signature (\texttt{OBS.TRANSDUCTION.01} as the
readout-side principle).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The statistical demand.}  A joint likelihood over the frozen
registry with: the full covariance matrix induced by the shared atoms; the \emph{effective}
number of degrees of freedom (not the raw hit count); a prespecified null space and
formula-selection rule; and a genuine holdout dataset (future measurements only).  \emph{Honest
reading of the existing null test:} the ``13/13, $<10^{-30}$'' Monte-Carlo statement (\vref{v100},
\texttt{docs/FALSIFICATION.md}) quantifies the look-elsewhere burden \emph{under its declared
null model} and is only interpretable as evidence to the extent that the null space, the formula
grammar, and all development data were prespecified --- which for historically known observables
they were not and cannot retroactively be.  The 2026-06-09 freeze (\vref{v84}) genuinely
protects \emph{future} measurements; it does not turn historically known data into blind
predictions.  The wording of \texttt{docs/FALSIFICATION.md} is adjusted accordingly in the same
change (honest, not weakened: the freeze and the kill tests stand).  Status:
\texttt{PRED.JOINTLIKELIHOOD.01} \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, promote round): v1 of the joint
likelihood is executed
(\veri{v992\_\allowbreak joint\_\allowbreak likelihood\_\allowbreak v1.py}; the contract stays
\mO).}
Nine frozen pull-matched scorecard triplets (no \texttt{experiments/} import at suite runtime):

TFPT at the $0$th percentile of $200$ scrambled decoders, leave-one-bundle-out stable.
Holdouts declared not consumed: JUNO $\Delta m^2$, DESI $\Sigma m_\nu$, DUNE $\delta_{\mathrm{CP}}$,
LiteBIRD $r$.  v1 declarations: $\Sigma_{\mathrm{exp}}$ diagonal (NuFIT/Planck covariances

Display marker stays \mO.

\section*{Research Programme --- the TFPT4D master route (one 4D transfer object instead of twenty formulas)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Programme: the TFPT4D master route}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A programme section (registered 2026-08-27 from the external bird's-eye
review), \emph{not} a claim: it organizes the existing 4D contracts and the four new ones below
into one route and moves \emph{no} marker.  The organizing observation: TFPT is probably not
missing twenty independent formulas; it is missing \emph{one} mathematical growth principle that
unfolds the discrete structure into a local, unitary, reflection-positive 4D quantum world.  The
discrete theory is then not spacetime itself but its \emph{genetic code}; the missing bridge
must produce spatial locality, reversible quantum dynamics, chiral matter, gauge fields, a
controlled continuum limit and concrete observables \emph{simultaneously}.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The master formulation (working hypothesis, unproven).}
\emph{The physical world is the unique local, reflection-positive and approximately
quantum-Markovian 4D completion of the TFPT seam algebra that is anomaly-free and has exactly
one relevant dimensionful direction.}  ``Exactly one dimensionful direction'' is the No-Unit
theorem already in force: the dimensionless compiler cannot produce an absolute scale, and
$v_{\rm geo}$ is the declared calibration torsor (\texttt{ANCHOR.VGEO.02}, \vref{v153},
\vref{v725}) --- the master route inherits this, it does not re-open it.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The seven conditions, mapped onto contracts.}
\textbf{(1)}~seam compatibility --- the boundary restriction of the 4D theory reproduces the
TFPT seam data \emph{exactly}, not only dimensions or group labels
(\texttt{TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01} gate T3 below);
\textbf{(2)}~reflection positivity / positive transfer operator (gate T2;
\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} demand O1);
\textbf{(3)}~a local unitary dilation of the observed Markov dynamics,
$\Phi(\rho)=\mathrm{Tr}_E[V(\rho\otimes\sigma_E)V^\dagger]$, with size-uniform locality and a
non-divergent Lieb--Robinson velocity (\texttt{DYN.UNITARY.DILATION.01});
\textbf{(4)}~chiral anomaly freedom with no mirror sector and the correct index
(\texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01});
\textbf{(5)}~only one relevant scale direction --- no hidden tunable dimensionless parameter
(kill test in \texttt{TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01}; $v_{\rm geo}$ calibrates the unit only);
\textbf{(6)}~approximate quantum-Markov recovery,
$I(A{:}C\mid B)\le C_0\,e^{-\mathrm{dist}(A,C)/\xi}$, so the bulk is quasi-locally
reconstructable from overlapping boundary data --- the small-CMI/recovery-map link is
Fawzi--Renner (arXiv:1410.0664), and local Markov properties of Gibbs states are established in
Chen--Rouz\'e (arXiv:2504.02208) (\texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01} route input);
\textbf{(7)}~nontriviality --- at least one connected four-point function and ultimately a
nontrivial S-matrix survive the limit (gate T7; \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01} demand B6).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The dynamics correction (discrete $\to$ dynamics).}  The finite
transport object already embeds exactly into a continuous Markov semigroup
(\texttt{DYN.MARKOV.EMBED.01} \mE\ at kernel level, \vref{v971}) --- but a Markov process is
irreversible and cannot be the fundamental dynamics.  The fundamental order must be
\emph{local unitary amplitudes $\to$ decoherence/partial trace $\to$ Markov matrix $T$}.  This
matches the now machine-checked finding that the classical unistochastic matrix $B_{ij}=|U_{ij}|^2$
keeps probabilities but loses the orientation sign of the Jarlskog invariant $J=\pm1/27$
(\veri{v977\_transfer\_unistochastic\_wilson.py}; the Birkhoff-fibre circulation parameter $t$ is a
second, independent classically hidden datum, \veri{v976\_seam\_lift\_birkhoff.py}): the fundamental
variable must carry a complex
holonomy/amplitude, which makes Wilson loops $W_\gamma=\mathrm{Tr}\prod_{e\in\gamma}U_e$ more
natural than bare transition probabilities.  Discrete Weyl dynamics on unitary cellular automata
show such a continuum transition is possible in principle (Bia\l ynicki-Birula,
arXiv:hep-th/9304070); for TFPT the discrete collision/QCA leg is constructed exactly
(\veri{v984\_markov\_qca\_dilation.py}), while the continuum/OS legs remain to be constructed
(\texttt{DYN.UNITARY.DILATION.01}).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Programme ordering (the efficient sequence).}
\textbf{(P1)}~the conditional 4D dimension selector --- \emph{done at conditional level}
(\texttt{DIMENSION.SELECTOR.4D.01} \mC, \veri{v975\_dimension\_selector\_4d.py}, next section);
\textbf{(P2)}~the single simple-current/spin-field generator
(\texttt{SEAM.SIMPLECURRENT.GENERATOR.01} \mO);
\textbf{(P3)}~the explicit finite 4D lattice action with its seven finite gates
(\texttt{TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01} \mO);
\textbf{(P4)}~the local unitary dilation with size-uniform Lieb--Robinson bounds
(\texttt{DYN.UNITARY.DILATION.01} \mO);
\textbf{(P5)}~the continuum/OS package (\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01} \mO);
\textbf{(P6)}~all four transfer bridges from one generating functional
(\texttt{FTRANSFER.GENERATING.01} \mO);
\textbf{(P7)}~gravitation strictly downstream --- modular Hamiltonians $\to$ entanglement
geometry $\to$ effective Einstein dynamics, in the assumption-declared discipline of
\texttt{GRAV.NONCIRCULAR.01} (Jacobson's entanglement equilibrium, arXiv:1505.04753,
\emph{presupposes} a local QFT and can never be cited as producing one);
\textbf{(P8)}~the prespecified joint likelihood with holdout
(\texttt{PRED.JOINTLIKELIHOOD.01} \mO);
\textbf{(P9)}~all three gauge couplings as unique stationary points of one nonabelian
zeta-det/inflow functional (\texttt{GAUGE.DETLINE.FIXPOINT.01} \mO; generalizes
\texttt{ALPHA.QUILLEN.EXACT.01}; honest: $\alpha_s(M_Z)$ is currently an external input);
\textbf{(P10)}~massless transversal spin-2 emergence from the same spectral determinant
(\texttt{GRAV.SPIN2.EMERGENCE.01} \mO; Einstein equation stays downstream/conditional;
\texttt{GRAV.NONCIRCULAR.01} binding);
\textbf{(P11)}~the Schwinger--Keldysh completion $(\,S,\rho_0\,)$ of cosmological transfers
(\texttt{FTRANSFER.SK.RHO0.01} \mO; \texttt{FTRANSFER.GENERATING.01} covers equilibrium only;
typed candidate $\theta_i=3\pi/5$, $\mu_4$ orientation picks $k=0$).
The Yang--Mills mass gap is \emph{not} defined away: it remains the open Clay problem; at best
the route reduces it to a concrete, structured lattice theory.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Kill criteria (the route is allowed to die early).}
No local reflection-positive finite 4D model with the correct seam boundary theory --- master
route dead.  The simple-current generator is nonlocal or fails to converge --- seam route dead.
The chiral measure term stays nonlocal or a global anomaly survives --- chiral route dead.
Mirror fermions do not decouple --- Standard-Model route dead.  The Lieb--Robinson velocity or
correlation length diverges uncontrolled --- no local continuum limit.  All connected four-point
functions vanish --- only a (generalized-)free theory.  Extra freely tunable dimensionless
parameters are needed --- the strong parameter-freeness claim is refuted.  The uniform twistor
flat direction (the sector-0 Okubo admixture of \veri{v973\_seam\_route\_narrowing.py}) shifts
\emph{normalized} observables rather than only the vacuum normalization of $Z$ --- a genuine
undetermined modulus.

\section*{Verified Selector --- \texttt{DIMENSION.SELECTOR.4D.01} (why $d=4$, conditionally)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Verified Selector: DIMENSION.SELECTOR.4D.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A \emph{conditional} identity \mC\ (ledger row
\texttt{DIMENSION.SELECTOR.4D.01}), machine-checked in
\veri{v975\_dimension\_selector\_4d.py} ($16/16$, sympy-exact).  The $\mu_4$ structure of the
compiler must \emph{not} be read as a proof of four spacetime dimensions
(\texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01}); what \emph{is} checkable is a selector theorem under a
named axiom set.  \textbf{Statement.}  Under
\textbf{A1}~$d>2$ (propagating local gauge fields, $d-2\ge1$ transverse polarizations),
\textbf{A2}~a dimensionless Yang--Mills coupling,
\textbf{A3}~real self-dual/anti-self-dual 2-form sectors (the Euclidean Hodge star an involutive
endomorphism of 2-forms), and
\textbf{A4}~chiral Weyl representations (even $d$),
the dimension $d=4$ is \emph{unique and minimal} on the exact scan window $d=2..12$ --- and the
selector is \emph{overdetermined}: A2 alone pins $\{4\}$ ($[g_{\rm YM}]=(4-d)/2$ forced
symbolically from dimensionless-action power counting) and A3 alone pins $\{4\}$
($*:\Omega^2\to\Omega^{d-2}$ is a self-map iff $d=4$; the explicit $6\times6$ star on
$\Lambda^2(\mathbb R^4)$ satisfies $*^2=I$ with eigenvalues $\pm1$ at multiplicities $3/3$ ---
the real $\mathfrak{su}(2)_+\times\mathfrak{su}(2)_-$ split), while A1\,\&\,A4 alone give
$\{4,6,8,10,12\}$: chirality does \emph{not} select $4$ by itself (honest ablation ledger).
Weyl chirality is certified by exact Clifford algebras $d=2..7$ (even $d$: $\gamma_*$
anticommutes with every $\gamma_\mu$, $\gamma_*^2=I$, $\mathrm{tr}\,\gamma_*=0$; odd $d$: the
full product is central and scalar --- no grading).  Signature is typed honestly: in Lorentzian
signature $*^2=-1$ on 2-forms in $d=4$, so the \emph{real} split is a Euclidean/OS-side
statement.  Mutants are caught (the wrong power counting $[g]=(6-d)/2$ selects $\{6\}$; the
``A4 alone selects 4'' overclaim is refused).  \textbf{Honest boundary.}  $\mu_4$ and
$g_{\rm car}$ enter nowhere --- deliberately, because ``$\mu_4\Rightarrow$ 4D'' is exactly the
forbidden reading.  This is a selector, \emph{not} a construction and \emph{not} a compiler
derivation: \emph{axiom provenance is the open half} --- if A1--A4 cannot themselves be forced
from $\{c_3, g_{\rm car}\}$, the selector remains a consistency statement, not a prediction
(the registered kill criterion).  Status: \texttt{DIMENSION.SELECTOR.4D.01} \mC; every 4D
construction contract stays \mO.

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{SEAM.SIMPLECURRENT.GENERATOR.01} (one generator instead of 128 currents)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: SEAM.SIMPLECURRENT.GENERATOR.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row
\texttt{SEAM.SIMPLECURRENT.GENERATOR.01}), \emph{not} a claim.  \textbf{Context.}  The seam
coverage carve (\veri{v973\_seam\_route\_narrowing.py}) shows the $\mathfrak{so}(16)_1$
fermion-bilinear half of the seam target is covered by the cited scaling-limit theorem; the
named residual is N1 (the $\mu_4$/GSO extension $SO(16)_1\to(E_8)_1$ as a \emph{net} statement
--- the 128 spinor currents are not bilinears), N2 (the twist/spin-field sector), N3 (the
one-sided chiral edge) and N4 (the holomorphy selector).  \textbf{Demand (the compression).}
Construct \emph{one} normalized simple-current/spin-field intertwiner --- the generator of the
index-4 Q-system extension (\vref{v125}/\vref{v154}) --- and prove
\textbf{(G1)}~convergence on the finite-particle core with energy bounds,
\textbf{(G2)}~locality relative to the bilinear net,
\textbf{(G3)}~the correct braiding/monodromy relation, and
\textbf{(G4)}~Q-system closure,
so that symmetry and fusion generate the \emph{entire} 128-dimensional extension sector from the
single generator --- collapsing N1\,+\,N2 (and, via uniqueness of the extension, the N4
selector) onto \emph{one} convergence theorem instead of 128 separate current controls.
\textbf{Kill tests.}  The generator is provably nonlocal relative to the bilinear net; the
energy bounds fail on the core; the braiding closes on the wrong extension ($SO(16)_1$ stays
unextended); the convergence rate is incompatible with the measured bilinear collar rate
($p=3.48$ inside the preregistered band, \veri{v973\_seam\_route\_narrowing.py}).
\emph{Dimension typing (binding):} hypotheses and conclusion live in $1{+}1$D --- closing this
contract does \emph{not} close \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01}.  Status:
\texttt{SEAM.SIMPLECURRENT.GENERATOR.01} \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, external master-object review wave
2): the generator is \emph{identified} and its lattice-arithmetic shadow is complete
(the analytic half G1--G4 stays \mO).}
\veri{v983\_\allowbreak simple\_\allowbreak current\_\allowbreak generator.py}
The
candidate is the glue vector $\lambda=(\omega_s,\omega_f)$ --- the $D_5$ spinor weight plus the
$A_3$ fundamental weight, in explicit rational coordinates --- with
$\|\omega_s\|^2=\tfrac54$, $\|\omega_f\|^2=\tfrac34$, $\|\lambda\|^2=2$ and conformal weight
$h_\lambda=\|\lambda\|^2/2=1$ (integer $\Rightarrow$ trivial self-monodromy, the bosonic
simple-current precondition).  Machine-checked exactly: $[\lambda]$ has \emph{order four} in
the discriminant group $\mathbb Z_4\times\mathbb Z_4$ of $D_5\oplus A_3$; $\lambda\cdot
L_0\subset\mathbb Z$ (isotropic glue, the glued lattice is even); $\det=16/4^2=1$ (unimodular
rank $8$, hence $E_8$; explicit certificate \vref{v1}); the coset root census is
$[52,64,60,64]$ --- with the rank-8 Cartan sector exactly
$248=(45{+}15)+(16,4)+(10,6)+(\overline{16},\bar 4)$ --- so the \emph{odd} fusion powers of the
one class $[\lambda]$ carry $64+64=128$, the full missing spinor sector.  Kill tests fire: the
vector-class glue (norm $7/4$) and the mismatched glue (norm $9/4$) both break evenness.  The
holomorphy \emph{shadow} is pinned at the lattice level ($\det 1\Rightarrow$ one sector, vs
$\det D_8=4\Rightarrow$ four sectors for $SO(16)_1$) --- the operator-algebraic N4 statement
is untouched.  Net effect: the contract's search space collapses from ``find a generator'' to
``prove G1--G4 for \emph{this} $\Psi_\lambda=Y(e^{(\omega_s,\omega_f)},z)$''; no marker moves.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, promote round): S3 exact $+$ the
quantitative S1/S2 skeleton $+$ the lemma reduction
(\veri{v988\_\allowbreak psi\_\allowbreak lambda\_\allowbreak reduction.py}; \emph{not} a
closure).}
S3: the crossed-product/tower exchange is exact (naturality; a non-equivariant mutant fails).

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, review wave 3): the $D{+}A$ architecture

UNCHANGED).}

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, completeness wave): 5 of 7 MMST
identification criteria executed, and C6 is the written-out hand-off
(\veri{v994\_\allowbreak mmst\_\allowbreak criteria\_\allowbreak battery.py}; display stays \mO).}
C1: measured $c=8.005$ (16 Majorana copies; one-cut convention $S_{M,1\mathrm{cut}}=S_{\mathrm{scalar}}/4$).
C2: conformal edge spectrum $[0.506,1.516,2.523,3.524]$ with degeneracies $[1,0,1,1,2]$, $v\approx0.988$.
