\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01} (the explicit finite 4D lattice action and its seven finite gates)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row \texttt{TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01});
route step (2) of the \texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01} ordering made concrete --- the
single object on which nearly all open 4D questions become simultaneously testable.
\textbf{Demand.}  Exhibit one fully explicit finite family of 4D Euclidean lattice theories
\[
\mathcal Z_a[J]=\int\mathcal DU\,\mathcal D\psi\,\mathcal D\Phi\;
e^{-S_a[U,\psi,\Phi]+J\mathcal O},
\qquad
S_a=S_{\rm Wilson}[U]+\bar\psi\,D_{\rm GW}[U,\Phi]\,\psi+S_\Phi[U,\Phi]+S_{\rm seam/top},
\]
where $U$ are the fundamental holonomies (TFPT fixes representation content, charge lattice,
coupling relations and boundary conditions), $D_{\rm GW}$ is a Ginsparg--Wilson/overlap operator
for the TFPT chiral representation content (the \texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01} operator), $\Phi$
is the \emph{only} allowed relevant scalar/link-mixing sector (EWSB downstream), and
$S_{\rm seam/top}$ is the boundary/topological module transporting the seam data into the bulk
--- first at finite lattice spacing $a$, deliberately \emph{without} continuum claims.
\textbf{Finite gates} (all machine-checkable at finite size; the promotion criteria for any
candidate): \textbf{(T1)}~exact gauge invariance; \textbf{(T2)}~a positive transfer matrix (RP
at finite $a$); \textbf{(T3)}~the seam boundary restriction reproduces the TFPT seam data
\emph{exactly}; \textbf{(T4)}~anomaly bookkeeping including the global $SU(2)$ anomaly;
\textbf{(T5)}~the index/generation count; \textbf{(T6)}~the mirror spectrum separated;
\textbf{(T7)}~at least one \emph{nonvanishing} connected four-point function.
\textbf{Kill tests.}  No local reflection-positive action with the correct seam restriction
exists (master route dead); every candidate is generalized-free (T7 unreachable); a hidden
freely tunable dimensionless parameter is required (the strong parameter-freeness claim is
refuted); the uniform twistor flat direction shifts \emph{normalized} observables rather than
only the vacuum normalization of $\mathcal Z$ (a genuine undetermined modulus).
\textbf{Relation.}  The continuum limit is the separate contract \texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01};
the unitary dilation is \texttt{DYN.UNITARY.DILATION.01}; $d=4$ is typed by
\texttt{DIMENSION.SELECTOR.4D.01} \mC; the generating-functional collapse is
\texttt{FTRANSFER.GENERATING.01}.  Chiral lattice state of the art is calibrated in
\texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01} (L\"uscher's exactly gauge-invariant abelian construction,
arXiv:hep-lat/9811032; the nonabelian anomaly analysis, arXiv:hep-lat/9904009; reflection
positivity proven for \emph{free} overlap fermions, Kikukawa--Usui arXiv:1005.3751 --- none of
this is the interacting chiral Standard Model).  Nothing in the corpus closes any gate.
Status: \texttt{TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01} \mO.

break is pinned on overlap time nonlocality.  The Hamiltonian route clears T2 by construction

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{SEAM.DETLINE.UNIFICATION.01} (the determinant-line unification hypothesis)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: SEAM.DETLINE.UNIFICATION.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research \emph{hypothesis} contract \mO\ (ledger row
\texttt{SEAM.DETLINE.UNIFICATION.01}) --- a precise conjecture, \emph{not} a result.
\textbf{Observation.}  Three separately open structures may be projections of \emph{one}
geometric object: \textbf{(1)}~the seam extension is described by a Q-system/simple current
(\texttt{SEAM.SIMPLECURRENT.GENERATOR.01}); \textbf{(2)}~the chiral fermion measure lives on a
determinant line (\texttt{CHIRAL4D.NOMIRROR.01} demand C1); \textbf{(3)}~anomalies and CP
phases appear as holonomies of that determinant line --- the \emph{finite} Quillen identity is
already two-sided (\veri{v974\_alpha\_faces\_computed.py}: Berry integral $=$
Poincar\'e--Lelong divisor integral $=+1$), and the continuum Bismut--Freed identification is
the named missing step of \texttt{ALPHA.QUILLEN.EXACT.01}.  \textbf{Demand (the compatibility
theorem).}
\[
\operatorname{Res}_{\rm seam}\det D_{4D}\;\cong\;\det D_{\rm seam}
\]
as line bundles \emph{with connection}, and the holonomy of the bulk determinant line equals
the discrete TFPT orientation phase (the classically invisible $\operatorname{sgn}J$
orientation bit, now machine-checked at matrix level with the Wilson-plaquette interference
readout as its minimal observable, \veri{v977\_transfer\_unistochastic\_wilson.py}; the
bulk-determinant identification demanded here stays open).  If such a theorem exists, seam extension, chiral
anomaly freedom, the generation index and the discrete CP orientation become different
projections of the same object.  \textbf{Kill tests.}  The two determinant lines differ by a
nontrivial local factor no counterterm removes; the bulk holonomy disagrees with the discrete
orientation phase on a computable family.  \emph{Honest boundary:} the continuum
Quillen/Bismut--Freed identification is exactly the critical missing step; nothing in the
corpus proves any part of the 4D side.  Status: \texttt{SEAM.DETLINE.UNIFICATION.01} \mO.

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{FTRANSFER.GENERATING.01} (four transfer bridges from one generating functional)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: FTRANSFER.GENERATING.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row \texttt{FTRANSFER.GENERATING.01});
the \emph{constructive successor} named by the \texttt{FR.TRANSFER.01} typing guard.  The four
continuous-transfer interfaces ($F_{\rm pole}$: source masses $\to$ pole, \vref{v183};
$F_{\rm Boltzmann}$: CP source $\to\eta_B$, \vref{v169}/\vref{v184}; $F_{\rm relic}$:
$(f_a,m_a,\theta_i,N_{\rm DW})\to\Omega_a$, \vref{v185}; $F_{\rm QCD}$:
$(\alpha_s,b_3,\Lambda_{\rm QCD},\text{lattice})\to m_p/m_e$, \vref{v164}) are currently four
\emph{separate} bridges.  \textbf{Demand.}  If the 4D theory $\mathcal Z_a[J]$ of
\texttt{TFPT4D.LATTICE.ACTION.01} exists with a controlled continuum limit
(\texttt{QFT4D.OS.RECON.01}), the four bridges must arise from \emph{one} generating functional
$W[J]=\log Z[J]$: lepton and quark masses as poles of two-point functions (zeros of
$\Gamma^{(2)}$); $m_p/m_e$ from the baryon and electron correlator poles of the \emph{same}
theory; $\eta_B$ from the real-time Schwinger--Keldysh dynamics of the \emph{same} theory (CP
violation, $B$ violation, nonequilibrium); the axion relic from the cosmological solution of
the \emph{same} normalized action.  $F_{\rm transfer}$ is then not another $3\times3$ matrix;
it is the full $W[J]$.  \textbf{Binding.}  The \texttt{FR.TRANSFER.01} guard stays in force:
the four frontier observables remain \mC/\mO-typed until $W[J]$ exists and each bridge is
\emph{derived} from it --- this contract permits no marker upgrade; it names the single object
whose existence would collapse the four transfer problems into computations within one theory.
\textbf{Kill test.}  The four bridges provably require mutually inconsistent normalizations of
the same $W[J]$.  Status: \texttt{FTRANSFER.GENERATING.01} \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-28, promote round): a finite $W[J]$ is
executed at $1{+}1$D $\mathbb Z_6$
(\veri{v990\_\allowbreak wj\_\allowbreak transduction\_\allowbreak mechanism.py}; the
\texttt{FR.TRANSFER.01} guard stays binding --- no upgrade).}
Content shadow: the real $6Y$ table as a $\mathbb Z_6$ centre.  Derivative identities:
$\partial W/\partial J=\langle O\rangle$ (residual $6.3\times10^{-11}$), Hessian $=$ Kubo
(residual $1\times10^{-7}$).  The four transfer bridges remain un-derived; this is the
finite-level mechanism, not $W[J]$ of the 4D theory.  Display marker stays \mO.

\veri{v1024\_\allowbreak parent\_\allowbreak internal\_\allowbreak boundaries.py}; no parent
marker move).}
A uniform full-parent gap $\Delta>0$ excludes a massless pole with
$\omega(k_{\min})\to0$.  Away from $k=0$ the symmetric-traceless target has five
pointwise components and the transverse-traceless projector has rank two, but its
direction-dependent $k\to0$ limit prevents an exact strictly finite-range direct TT
projector.  A viable escape therefore requires a critical physical sequence together
with local tensor gauge constraints whose Ward identities generate the TT sector only
in the infrared.  The finite representation count is not a derived TFPT stress
operator, and no massless pole, universal coupling, diffeomorphism Ward identity or
gravitational continuum theorem is constructed.  \texttt{GRAV.SPIN2.EMERGENCE.01},
T7 and TOE remain \mO.

\subsection*{TEL-B reduction (2026-09-04)}
\noindent The residual \texttt{TEL-B-EXTERNAL} is restated below.  The earlier scalar

but is no longer the route.  Numerically closed
($1.7833+0.7863=2.57<3$; conditional analytic $2.9495<3$ with $c_E=0.30$);
round 3 closed C1, C2a and the interval-TV input. Round 4 below now closes
CF, DG and the relaxed uniform TEL-B norm, including the finite-grid aliases:
$\|R_N\|_{\rm HS}<2.995906<3$ for all even $N\ge16$.
ALG-EXH and the charged MMST identification remain open.

Evidence (experiments, not \texttt{veri}-citable):
\texttt{experiments/tfpt-discovery/mmst\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak trace\_\allowbreak decomposition\_\allowbreak probe.py},
\texttt{mmst\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak cover\_\allowbreak split\_\allowbreak probe.py},
\texttt{mmst\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak bound\_\allowbreak a\_\allowbreak probe.py} ($9/9$),
\texttt{mmst\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak bound\_\allowbreak b\_\allowbreak probe.py} ($17/20$, G18--G20 open by design).

the new load-bearing certificate is
\veri{v1026\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak round4\_\allowbreak closure.py}.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Cover identity and sawtooth split.}
Corpus convention $e^{-ipx}$, $\alpha=\tfrac14$, $M=4N$, $d=x-y$.  The four-fold
cover identity is
\begin{equation*}
  (\Gamma_0-\Gamma_\alpha)(x,y)
  =(1-i)\,C_M(d+N)+2\,C_M(d+2N)+(1+i)\,C_M(d+3N).
\end{equation*}
The remainder splits as $R_N=R_N^{\mathrm{sm}}+M_N$ with
$M_N=-(S_N+H_N)\otimes Q$.  The scalar sawtooth response has the closed form
\begin{equation*}
  s_N(x,y)=\frac{-(1+t^2)+3it-it^3}{4N(1-t^2)},\qquad
  t=\tan\frac{\pi(x-y)}{4N}.
\end{equation*}
Triangle target: $\lVert R_N\rVert\le\lVert R_N^{\mathrm{sm}}\rVert+\lVert M_N\rVert<3$.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Bound (A) --- native interval input closed
(\texttt{SEAM.MMST.TELB.ROUND3.01};
\veri{v1022\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak round3\_\allowbreak certificates.py}).}
The continuous family $F=P-\mathrm{saw}\cdot dP$ has vanishing derivative jump
($\mathrm{rank}\,dF'=0$, $\lVert dF'\rVert=3.4{\times}10^{-10}$); the first
discontinuity sits in $F''$ (rank~$4$, norm $2.000000029$).  The $2{\times}2$
crossing lemma supplies the removable crossing chart.  The all-mode BV bound
$\lvert\hat F(m)\rvert\le\mathrm{TV}(F'')/(2\pi\lvert m\rvert)^3$ with
$256$-bit Arb/Acb arithmetic on $5812$ rational cells gives
\[
 \mathrm{TV}_p(F'')\le15.8176959629889243576\ldots<15.928674,
 \qquad K_3^{\mathrm{TV}}<2.517464500835.
\]
The cut contribution has exact Hilbert--Schmidt norm $2$; the remaining cells use
validated eigensystems, Taylor enclosures and an analytic ninth-derivative majorant.
Thus the former float64/Richardson constant is retired. Round 4 additionally
certifies the infinite Fourier core below $1.783260551$ and the finite-grid
alias contribution below $0.017333$. The actual uniform smooth remainder
bound is $\|R_N^{\rm sm}\|_{\rm HS}<1.800594$; the older finite $1.7833$
claim was not justified by the limiting core and is withdrawn.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Bound (B) --- historical reduction; closed in round 4 below.}
Relaxed target $\lVert S_N+H_N\rVert^2\le1.2167^2/2=0.740179$ (measured limit
$0.5913$).  Write $S_N+H_N=A_\infty+\mathrm{Res}_N$ with
$\lVert A_\infty\rVert^2=\tfrac54-8/\pi^2$ exact.  Lemma~6 cross term
${}\le0.3813$ (all even $N\le512$, and $1024/2048/4096$).  Needed
$\sum\lvert\mathrm{Res}_N\rvert^2\le0.276873914$; measured max $0.154983791$.
Master identity (divided differences via the cotangent partial fraction; weight
$\{(m+\alpha)/N\}$ is $N$-periodic; residual $2.3{\times}10^{-15}$).
One-variable reduction
\begin{equation*}
  N\cdot\mathrm{Res}_N(k,l)
  =r(u,v)
  +A\frac{(1-\lambda)\,\Delta\eta-\lambda\,\Delta\varphi_N}{v-u}
\end{equation*}
(residual $1.16{\times}10^{-14}$), with $u=k/N$, $v=l/N$, $A=\sin^2(\pi\alpha)/\pi^2$,
and
\begin{align*}
  \eta(k)
  &=-\psi(k+1-\alpha)+\log\lvert k\rvert+\pi\cot(\pi\alpha)\,1_{k<0},\\
  \varphi_N(k)
  &=\Phi_N(k)-\log N-\gamma-\Phi_\star(k/N),\\
  \Phi_\star(u)
  &=\log\Bigl\lvert\frac{u}{\tan\pi u}\Bigr\rvert
    -\pi\!\!\int_{u-1/2}^{u+1/2}\!\!\sigma\cot(\pi\sigma)\,d\sigma
    -\pi u\cot(\pi\alpha).
\end{align*}
Closed bounds $\pi^2/3\le\Phi_\star''\le\pi^2-4$.  The kernel $r$ is singular
only on $u=0$, $v=0$.  The former offset midpoint values were
$C_\star=0.152044949/0.152045069$; the native certificate below replaces them
as the load-bearing bound.
Cell averages $(\mathcal K_N)_{kl}:=N\iint_{Q_{kl}}r$ obey
$\lVert\mathcal K_N\rVert^2\le C_\star$ exactly (Cauchy--Schwarz on each cell).
Measured $\sqrt N\,\lVert E_N\rVert=0.1943\ldots0.2013$ ($N=16\ldots512$).
Required $c_E\le0.542702$, margin $2.70$.  Assembled
$(\sqrt{0.1525}+0.2013/4)^2=0.1943<0.2769$.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{C1 and C2a closed (2026-09-05;
\texttt{SEAM.MMST.TELB.C1C2A.01};
\veri{v1025\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak c1\_\allowbreak c2a\_\allowbreak certificates.py}).}
For C1, the exact simplification of the real kernel is integrated over $3888$
analytic rectangles at $100$-bit precision.  Logarithmic charts remove the two
axis singularities, a removable seam chart treats the opposite-sign corner, and
the omitted axis union is bounded by $3.1570760454{\times}10^{-7}$.  Parseval
supplies the exact imaginary contribution $1/12$, giving the outward enclosure
\[
 C_\star\le0.1520453048\ldots<0.1525.
\]
For C2a, exact cotangent reindexing gives the $3/4$-point Peano identity
\[
 \varphi_N(k+1)-\varphi_N(k)
 =h f((k+3/4)h)-\int_{kh}^{(k+1)h}f(u)\,du,
 \qquad h=N^{-1}.
\]
The partial-fraction formula
$f'(u)=\sum_{m\in\mathbb Z\setminus\{0\}}(u-m)^{-2}$ implies
$\pi^2/3\le f'\le\pi^2-4$ and makes the increments monotone.  Shifted Euler
summation with its periodic $B_3$ remainder reduces the two endpoints to outward
constant comparisons in v1025:
\[
 N\sup_k\lvert\varphi_N(k)\rvert
 \le0.508228779140631\ldots<0.509<0.51
 \qquad(N\ge16\text{ even}).
\]

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{C2b scalar reduction and the former remaining target
(\veri{v1022\_\allowbreak telb\_\allowbreak round3\_\allowbreak certificates.py}).}
The analytic one-dimensional reduction, with its final arithmetic enclosed by Arb,
gives
\[
 \begin{aligned}
 c_\varepsilon&=0.288547359238391\ldots,\\
 c_\zeta&=0.110677064900715\ldots,\\
 c_{\rm potential}&=0.399224424139106\ldots<0.400.
 \end{aligned}
\]
The executable encloses $1\le n\le15$ and checks positive-coefficient rational
tail comparisons for $n\ge16$.  The sign-controlled digamma and midpoint
inequalities which justify those tails are analytic lemmas of this proof, not an
independent formalization inside the executable. The former CF/DG targets were
\[
 \sqrt N\,\lVert(K'_N-K_N)_{\rm offdiag}\rVert_{\mathrm{HS}}\le0.139,
 \qquad
 \sqrt N\,\lVert(\mathrm{Res}_N-K_N)_{\rm diag}\rVert_{\mathrm{HS}}\le0.060.
\]
The diagonal is Hilbert--Schmidt orthogonal to the off-diagonal, and these two
bounds suffice for the stated $c_E\le0.542702$ budget. Round 4 proves the
stronger constants $0.065697$ and $0.042959$, respectively, and gives
$c_E<0.466902$. The proof and interval arithmetic are in the round-4
supplement below and v1026; these are no longer open inputs.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{ALG2 correction (binding).}
The former Vandermonde-to-generated-algebra implication is withdrawn.  The exact
one-mode countermodel has full CAR algebra $M_2$, while the even/code-generated
subalgebra is diagonal; the annihilator stays at distance at least $1$ from it.
Fourier/Vandermonde inversion therefore supplies only full-CAR compression, not the
microscopic generated algebra.  ALG2 requires an independent FE-GEN
compression-surjectivity theorem or a precise gauge/code-projected microscopic
algebra argument.  The positive ALG1 estimate is likewise conditional on the
generated projection containing the microscopic low-energy sectors.
The round-4 sector/frame lemma gives a sufficient conditional interface, not
a microscopic FE-GEN proof. ALG-EXH and
\texttt{SEAM.MMST.TYPEIII.CHARGED.01} remain \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Retired routes (do not retry).}
Absolute-value majorants ($3\sqrt N$); global BV
majorant; per-cell log counterterms at $\pm\tfrac12$ (nothing to counterterm);
shifted-cell majorisation (ratio $0.73$); Szeg\H{o}/Widom split
(Fisher--Hartwig $\pi^{-2}\log N$ per summand); trace decomposition (each term
diverges $\propto\log N$, cancellation $2/\pi^2+2/\pi^2-4/\pi^2$).

\item \textbf{T1:} the finite axiom-core/W-bridge premises have executable

  dimension selector remain open.
\item \textbf{T2:} the relaxed uniform TEL-B norm is closed below $2.995906<3$
  at fixed $M=1$, $N_y=8$ for all even $N\ge16$, including C2b and finite-grid aliases. Microscopic
  one-edge generation, FE-GEN/ALG-EXH and MMST remain open; the sector/frame
  bridge is conditional and the old Vandermonde implication remains refuted.
  Round 7 constructs charged corners only after adding an auxiliary local
  $\Z_4$ flux register. The simple eight-channel quarter twist has
  $h=1/4$, not $h_\lambda=1$; the required microscopic dressing and
  gauge/code quotient have not been supplied.
\item \textbf{T3:} dynamics exists only at Hamiltonian-class level.  No shared
  complete $3{+}1$D parent or continuum theorem is constructed.
\item \textbf{T4:} exact canonical DET excitations and a signed $z=1$ wall
  exist under the stated singlet and fixed-classical-background premises.
  The source's factorized onsite model has gap at least $1+\sqrt3/2$;
  this bound is not an interacting hopping theorem. An ordinary bond
  violates the proposed vacuum-relative form bound, not every possible
  interacting gap argument. No hard bare-mirror gap, chiral quantum-link realization, measure or
  $4$D continuum theorem follows.
\item \textbf{T5:} finite IR witnesses and the v1020 spectator obstruction make
  prime completion binding, but OS reconstruction, confinement, clustering and
  nontrivial scattering remain open.
\item \textbf{T6:} full Gauss counts remove the blanket projected-dimension
  obstruction (one site $3\bmod5$, cube $0\bmod5$ with the specified nine-irrep
  link truncation and full $192$-mode site character), but give no canonical
  compatible clock family or neutrino mass texture. The symmetric candidate
  forbids every Majorana bilinear.
\item \textbf{T7:} the local first-class constraint target now has an explicit
  zero-mean Weyl/Fock quantization, a distributional continuum limit and a
  positive covariant local free curvature field of helicities $\pm2$.
  Its unstable periodic zero block must still be removed globally.
  Prescribed conserved sources obey the linear physical-Fourier Ward
  identities, but dynamical quantum sources and universal nonlinear
  coupling are not constructed. None of these target-field results
  supplies a common-parent TFPT embedding.
\item \textbf{T8:} a symmetric cap on the selected internal $12$D pair space has gap one, but the
  invariant space is two-dimensional; symmetry does not select its ray,
  and its vacuum-preserving extension selects no global $\rho_0$ or SK functional.
\end{itemize}
All T1--T8 gates and \texttt{TFPT.TOE.COMPLETE.01} remain \mO; no marker moves.
A single shared $3{+}1$D parent satisfying T3--T8 is still missing.

\section*{Research Contract --- \texttt{SEAM.MILNOR.LOCALRING.01} (canonical identification of the seam collar with the reduced Milnor algebra)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Research Contract: SEAM.MILNOR.LOCALRING.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} A research contract \mO\ (ledger row
\texttt{SEAM.MILNOR.LOCALRING.01}), registered 2026-08-30 from review wave~6.
(\veri{v1004\_\allowbreak milnor\_\allowbreak frobenius\_\allowbreak gray.py}.)
\textbf{Demand.}  A \emph{canonical} identification between the raw seam collar / $E_8$
transition bus and the reduced Milnor algebra $\mathbb F_2[y,z]/(y^2,z^4)$ preserving
\emph{simultaneously} the dual-number action, the Frobenius pairing, the Gray torsor, the
deck action and CP.  \textbf{Acceptance.}  The identification must be geometric/mechanical
(not basis-chosen) and must produce the rank clock and socle-CP as \emph{outputs}.
\textbf{Kill tests.}  Any second inequivalent identification with the same invariants; failure
of $\mu_4$-equivariance.  \textbf{Note.}  Would reduce P2 / alignment-bit / Gauss-code /
Brieskorn origin to one local ring.  Currently \mO\ because the Milnor algebra of
$x^2+y^3+z^5\cong\mathbb Q[y,z]/(y^2,z^4)$ (dimension $8$) and the spectral numbers
$=\{1,7,11,13,17,19,23,29\}$ (the $E_8$ exponents) are \emph{classical} (cite, no claim),
and the corpus map is unproven.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed finite algebra (review wave~6; the contract stays \mO).}
New-in-corpus exact \mE-finite: rank clock $(2-a)(4-b)$, table sum $30=h(E_8)$;
$S=pqr\cdot\mu/8$; self-clock iff $\mu=8$; $(2,3,5)$ unique among ordered spherical triples;
Galois--Gray action ($11$ swaps sheets, $7$ cycles the seam clock in binary Gray order
$0\to1\to3\to2$, $-1=(a,b)\mapsto(1-a,3-b)$); CP $=$ socle/Frobenius duality (residue pairing
nonzero iff $a+a'=1$, $b+b'=3$, $m+m'=30$); integral dual-number bridge
$\mathbb Z[y,z]/(y^2,z^4)\otimes\mathbb F_2$ free of rank $4$ over
$D=\mathbb F_2[\varepsilon]/(\varepsilon^2)$, $\mathbb Z[i]/(2)\cong D$, basis-dependent.
Interpretive \mC: $15=8_R+7_{NS}$ as $PG(3,2)=AG(3,2)\sqcup PG(2,2)$.
Must-fail: $(2,3,7)$ gives $S=63\neq42$; $A_8$ self-clock passes but the exponent fingerprint
fails; wrong socle rank $4$.
Status: \texttt{SEAM.MILNOR.LOCALRING.01} \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-30, afternoon harvest;
\veri{v1010\_\allowbreak simplicity\_\allowbreak bridge\_\allowbreak census.py};
canonical status SHARPENED, display stays \mO).}
The strong all-structure identification is finitely obstructed: at $\mathbb F_2$, clock
order $2$ versus $4$ and CP Jordan rank $2$ versus $4$ make the four-structure isomorphism
set empty; at every upstairs home the same obstruction persists ($\mathbb Z/4$: $J^2=-I$
versus Gray $P^2\neq-I$, $D_8$ versus $C_4\times C_2$; Gaussian $k=2$ order collapse,
$k=3$ rank/relation clash; affine conjugacy cannot repair the linear parts).
Only a coarse $D$-module$+$pairing bridge exists (witness constructed;
$\lvert\mathrm{Aut}(X)\rvert=36864$, $\lvert\mathrm{Aut}(Y)\rvert=8$).
The ring-internal identities of v1004 (rank clock, socle CP, self-clock) are
unaffected.  No marker move.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{P2 rehabilitation note (not a third contract;
\veri{v1005\_\allowbreak relative\_\allowbreak pencil\_\allowbreak bridges.py},
corrected by \veri{v1010\_\allowbreak simplicity\_\allowbreak bridge\_\allowbreak census.py}).}
The v1005 circularity flag is corrected as forced provenance: the bit-selector ladder

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-30, late-evening harvest;

W-bridge P-anch is DERIVED: $e_1=R^{-1}(1,1,2)^T$ uniquely via the winding/triple lock

\section*{Externalization Contract --- \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} (the seam scaling-limit theorem, packaged for external specialists)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Externalization Contract: SEAM.EQUIV.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} This section is a \emph{hand-off document}: it packages the one remaining
seam obligation as a self-contained problem statement for an outside specialist in conformal
nets / vertex operator algebras / algebraic QFT. It \emph{documents}, it does not close: the
ledger row \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} stays \mO\ (its conditional route \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.MMST.01}
is closed modulo cited theorems; the unconditional parent is the target below). Nothing in this
section moves a marker, and no claim beyond the corpus is made. Everything an external solver
needs --- hypotheses, runnable finite models, the Lean axiom surface, the negative controls a
valid solution must respect, and the exact acceptance criteria --- is listed here with its
verification module.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(1) Minimal theorem statement.} \emph{The scaling limit of the
gapped $\mu_4$-equivariant CAR collar exists in the local-net topology; the index-$4$
simple-current extension functor commutes with this limit; and the Osterwalder--Schrader
reconstruction of the limit state is isomorphic to the holomorphic $(E_8)_1$ lattice conformal
net.} Explicitly, with $\mathcal A_L$ the finite collar net ($D=16$ Majoranas, gap
$\Delta=6\ln(3/2)>0$, carrier clock $\mu_4$) and $\alpha_{\mu_4}$ the index-$4$ simple-current
extension (the Longo Q-system of \vref{v125}/\vref{v154}), the target decomposes into four
lemmata:
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{(L1) Uniform energy / nuclearity bounds.} The rescaled collar family satisfies
  uniform (in $L$) energy bounds and a nuclearity/compactness condition strong enough to extract
  a limit state on the quasi-local algebra. \mO\ --- the finite side supplies the uniform gap
  (\vref{v302}) and exact quasi-freeness (Wick/Pfaffian closure, \vref{v113}/\vref{v155});
  $N$-uniform CAR nuclearity is now proved as compactness input
  (\veri{v1016\_\allowbreak state\_\allowbreak gap\_\allowbreak batteries.py}:
  $\nu_N\le e^{16 C_\beta}$) but does \emph{not} identify $P_{\mathrm{gen}}$/ALG-EXH;
  L1 stays \mO.
\item \textbf{(L2) Convergence of local algebras.} The local algebras of the rescaled collar
  converge (in the sense of the Osborne--Stottmeister scaling-limit framework) to the local
  algebras of a chiral CFT of central charge $c=8$. The cited theorem covers free CAR lattice
  fermions in the range $\operatorname{rank}\le c\le D$; the collar sits at $8\le8\le16$
  (\vref{v356}/\vref{v366}). \mO\ as applied \emph{with the $\mu_4$-equivariant structure
  retained through the limit}. The coverage of the cited theorem is since carved exactly
  (\veri{v973\_seam\_route\_narrowing.py}): \emph{covered} is the $\mathfrak{so}(16)_1$
  fermion-bilinear current algebra (strong convergence, Virasoro and correlator control,
  polynomial error bounds); \emph{not covered} --- the named residual --- are N1 the
  $\mu_4$/GSO extension $SO(16)_1\to(E_8)_1$ as a net statement, N2 the twist/spin-field
  sector, N3 the one-sided chiral edge net, and N4 the OS/holomorphy selector; a measured
  collar convergence rate $p=3.48$ lies inside the preregistered band (the trivial $M{=}3$
  control does not converge).  Completeness wave
  (\veri{v994\_mmst\_criteria\_battery.py}): $5$ of $7$ MMST identification criteria are now
  executed at the finite level (measured $c=8.005$, $(E_8)_1$ vacuum character
  $[1,0,248,0,4124,0,34752]$, $k=1$, $\det D_8=4\to\det E_8=1$); C6 is the written-out
  quasi-free theorem, C7 stays cited.
\item \textbf{(L3) The crossed product commutes with the limit.} The index-$4$ simple-current
  extension (the $\mu_4$ Q-system, equivalently the crossed product by the glue
  $\Z_4$, \vref{v469}) commutes with the scaling limit:
  $\lim(\mathcal A_L\rtimes\mu_4)\;\cong\;(\lim\mathcal A_L)\rtimes\mu_4$. The finite Q-system
  is explicit and machine-verified (\vref{v125}/\vref{v154}); the commutation is \mO.
\item \textbf{(L4) Uniqueness of the holomorphic $c=8$ reconstruction.} The OS reconstruction of
  the limit is a \emph{holomorphic} ($\mu$-index $1$, one primary) $c=8$ net, hence the
  $(E_8)_1$ lattice net --- the same-central-charge rival $SO(16)_1$ ($\det K=4$, four
  primaries) is excluded by the order-$4$ clock, \emph{not} by $c=8$ alone
  (\vref{v351}/\vref{v378}). The classification input is cited (KLM/M\"uger; lattice-net
  uniqueness); that the \emph{reconstructed} net satisfies its hypotheses is \mO.
\end{itemize}
\emph{Dimension typing (per \texttt{DIMENSION.UPLIFT.FIREWALL.01}):} hypotheses \emph{and}
conclusion of L1--L4 live in $1{+}1$D --- the target is a chiral $c{=}8$ boundary object.  No
part of this contract, proved or not, closes a $3{+}1$D claim; the seam$\to$bulk uplift is the
separate contract \texttt{SEAM.BULK4D.RECON.01}.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(2) Complete hypothesis list.} Every input an external proof may
use, typed. \textsc{proven-finite} = machine-checked in the suite (module cited);
\textsc{cited-classical} = a published theorem, used as stated, never re-proved;
\textsc{open} = the target itself.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(3) Available finite models (runnable).} All models live in the
repository and run standalone (Python 3, \texttt{mpmath}/\texttt{sympy}; \texttt{python

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(4) Lean axiom surface (honest).} The Lean layer certifies the
\emph{logical composition} and the finite arithmetic --- \emph{not} the analysis. In
\texttt{TfptCarrier/SeamScalingLimit.lean} (\texttt{FORM.SEAM.MMST.01}) the kernel proves, by
\texttt{decide} with no axioms: the range $8\le8\le16$ (\texttt{mmst\_range}), chirality
$c_-=8\neq0$ (\texttt{chiral}) and the holomorphy discriminator $\det K$: $1$ vs $4$
(\texttt{holomorphic\_discriminator}). \emph{Axiomatized} (named, cited, not re-proved):
\texttt{mmst\_scaling\_limit}, \texttt{adamo\_holomorphic\_e8} and the structural input
\texttt{collar\_gapped}; the carriers \texttt{CollarGappedQuasifree}, \texttt{ChiralCFT\_c8},
\texttt{SeamIsE8} are \emph{opaque} \texttt{Prop}s. The same pattern holds in
\texttt{SeamEquivChain.lean} (\texttt{kitaev\_invertibility}, \texttt{mueger\_klm\_holomorphy},
\texttt{conway\_sloane\_e8} as axioms), \texttt{SeamApplicabilityLedger.lean} and
\texttt{BWKeystone.lean}. No \texttt{sorry} in any seam module; \texttt{\#print axioms} pins the
residual to exactly the named cited theorems. \textbf{Caveat stated plainly:} no operator
algebras, nets, or states are formalised --- L1--L4 have \emph{zero} Lean content beyond this
composition audit, so the formalisation is thinner than a casual reading of ``Lean-verified''
would suggest.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(5) Must-fail controls.} A submitted proof is accepted only if it
\emph{fails} on the following deformations (each already realised as a machine-checked negative
control):
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{MF-S1 ($c=8$ alone must not suffice).} Any argument that would equally conclude
  ``$SO(16)_1$'' is invalid: the proof must consume the order-$4$ clock / $\det K=1$
  discriminator, not just the central charge (the red-team point; \vref{v281}/\vref{v378}).
\item \textbf{MF-S2 (wrong extension index breaks it).} Replacing the index-$4$ $\mu_4$ glue by
  the index-$2$ $\Z_2$ glue must yield $SO(16)_1$ ($\mu=4$), i.e.\ the $E_8$
  conclusion must demonstrably fail (\vref{v154}/\vref{v351}).
\item \textbf{MF-S3 (trivial collar).} At the trivial parameter $M=3$ ($C=0$, clean gap, no
  edge) the construction must produce \emph{no} chiral limit (\vref{v367}/\vref{v368}).
\item \textbf{MF-S4 (marks).} A non-order-$4$ clock, or a mark configuration away from the free
  $\mu_4$ orbit, must break the $\tau=i$ pillowcase input (\vref{v216}/\vref{v217}).
\item \textbf{MF-S5 (firewall).} No circular import: the proof must not assume the $(E_8)_1$ net
  on the seam at any intermediate step (the Route-i/Route-ii import firewall, \vref{v286}).
\end{itemize}

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(6) Acceptance tests.} \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} counts as solved
exactly when: (i) L1--L4 are proved under the hypothesis list of (2), with any additional
hypothesis flagged explicitly; (ii) the composed theorem outputs the $(E_8)_1$ net --- one
primary, $248$ level-$1$ currents, torus degeneracy $1$ --- as a \emph{consequence}, matching
\vref{v377}/\vref{v378}; (iii) all five must-fail controls verifiably fail; (iv) no downstream
TFPT readout (in particular no $\alpha$ data) enters as an input. Partial results that close a
single lemma (e.g.\ L1 alone) narrow the target and are recorded as such; only the full chain
moves the ledger --- and any marker move happens in \texttt{status\_ledger.csv} by the house
process, never in this document.

\section*{Externalization Contract --- \texttt{ALPHA.QUILLEN.EXACT.01} (the determinant-line variation theorem, packaged for external specialists)}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Externalization Contract: ALPHA.QUILLEN.EXACT.01}
% =====================================================================
\noindent\emph{Typing.} The second hand-off document: the one remaining EM obligation, packaged
for an outside specialist in determinant lines / $\eta$-invariants / index theory. It
\emph{documents}, it does not close: \texttt{ALPHA.QUILLEN.EXACT.01} stays \mO\ (external math,
type A, \vref{v384}), and it is a \emph{face} of \texttt{SEAM.EQUIV.01} (\vref{v382}) --- not a
second independent gate. The \emph{value} $\alpha^{-1}$ is closed \mE\ regardless (unique root
$+$ ablation, \vref{v3}); open is only ``why \emph{this} functional''. Nothing here moves a
marker.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Relation (2026-08-28).}  The nonabelian upgrade of this $U(1)$
face is the new contract \texttt{GAUGE.DETLINE.FIXPOINT.01} (all three gauge couplings as unique
stationary points of one zeta-det/inflow functional; honest: $\alpha_s(M_Z)$ is currently an
external input).  This row stays the $U(1)$ face; that row is the three-coupling demand.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Relative-determinant note (2026-08-29, review wave 4).}
The correct object is the \emph{relative} determinant
$Q(A)=\log[\det'\Delta_A/\det'\Delta_{A_0}]+8\,b_1\,c_3^6\log\varphi(A)$;
the BFK constant $2^{-4}$ is $A/\tau/\alpha$-independent at fixed cut number, so
$\delta\log 2^{-4}=0$ --- no physical compensation needed (consistent with
\texttt{COMPENSATION\_INTERNAL} and the reviewer's correction).  Absolute normalisation is
fixed by the reference $A_0$.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(1) Minimal theorem statement.} Two steps, both over the moduli
of flat $U(1)$ connections on the $\mu_4$ seam (the twist torus):
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{(T1) Occupied Berry line and continuum determinant-line comparison.}
  \emph{Finite object.} Over the same twist torus $B$, specify the gapped collar
  Hamiltonians $H_L(b)$, their gauge identifications and
  $P_L(b)=\mathbf{1}_{(-\infty,0)}(H_L(b))$. The gap defines the smooth occupied
  bundle and its Berry line $L_{\mathrm{occ},L}=\det\operatorname{Ran}P_L$.
  Its FHS integer is $C_{\mathrm{FHS}}=+1$ within numerical tolerance at
  $L=4,6$ (\vref{v472}). For $\nabla=d+A_{\mathrm{occ}}$ with
  $A_{\mathrm{occ}}=\operatorname{tr}(U^\dagger dU)$ and $c_1=iF_{\mathrm{occ}}/(2\pi)$,
  the continuum Berry convention is $C_{\mathrm{Berry}}=\int_B F_{\mathrm{occ}}/(2\pi i)$,
  hence $c_1(L_{\mathrm{occ},L})[B]=-C_{\mathrm{Berry}}$.
  Identify the finite-mesh integer with this continuum value by a controlled
  mesh limit or an independent topological calculation; check duality before
  comparing its sign with a continuum theta-line convention.
  The gap check establishes no global nonvanishing section: such a section of
  this same line would force $c_1=0$, contradicting the nonzero Chern class.
  A global projector or ground-state ray is not a globally chosen ground-state vector.

  \emph{Continuum object.} Define independently a chiral elliptic Fredholm family
  $D_b^+$, including domains, coupling data and gauge descent, with Quillen line
  $L_Q=(\det\ker D^+)^{-1}\otimes\det\operatorname{coker}D^+$, Quillen metric
  and Bismut--Freed connection. Fix the chirality/duality convention in the comparison.
  The null-mode locus $\Theta\subset B$ is allowed and must be specified; a globally
  invertible $D^+$ has a trivial Fredholm determinant line and cannot supply $|c_1[B]|=1$.

  \emph{Comparison demand.} Construct the comparison between $L_{\mathrm{occ},L}$
  and $L_Q$ (or its explicitly specified dual) over the common base and prove
  convergence at the level of Hermitian metrics and connections after a fixed
  relative renormalisation. Norm-resolvent convergence and a Fermi gap alone are
  insufficient: uniform control of the high-frequency/heat-kernel contributions
  and their parameter variations is required. Match the curvature forms and the
  holonomies on the two independent twist cycles; equal integrated Chern numbers
  do not determine the connection. The reference $A_0$ fixes the remaining constant
  normalisation. On $B\setminus\Theta$ the logarithmic determinant variation is
  ordinary; its extension across $\Theta$ must retain the kernel and divisor data.

  \emph{Dai--Freed data.} A Dai--Freed vector requires an actual family of fillings
  and takes values in the pulled-back inverse boundary line. A global nonvanishing
  section may be asserted only on that pullback or on a specified anomaly-cancelled
  product, not on the uncompensated line with nonzero $c_1$. Alternatively, cylinders and
  mapping tori may define transport and holonomy without a global trivialisation.
  All continuum comparison statements remain \mO; the finite Berry/gap results
  of \vref{v472} are unchanged.
\item \textbf{(T2) Variation $=$ Bismut--Freed curvature $=$ inflow response, with the exact
  corpus coefficients.} On the continuum line of T1,
  \[
    \delta_\tau\Bigl(\log\det\nolimits_\zeta\Delta_{U(1)}\;+\;8\,b_1\,\cthree^{6}\,
    \log\varphi_{\mathrm{seam}}\Bigr)=0 ,
  \]
  i.e.\ the EM closure $F_{U(1)}(\alpha)=k_0\,\alpha^3-2\,\cthree^{3}\alpha^2-8\,b_1\,\cthree^{6}
  \ln(1/\varphi_{\mathrm{seam}})=0$ \emph{is} the stationarity of the exact
  $\zeta$-regularised Quillen functional, with every coefficient as it stands in the corpus:
  the Chern level $k_0=|C|=1$ (the computed bulk invariant of the S3 collar, normalised by the
  embedding index $k_Y=\operatorname{tr}(Y^2)/\operatorname{tr}(T_3^2)=5/3$, \vref{v470}); the
  Gauss--Bonnet boundary coefficient $\cthree=1/(8\pi)$ with heat-kernel powers
  $\cthree^{3},\cthree^{6}$ (\vref{v216}/\vref{v342}); the $U(1)_Y$ $a_4$/index coefficient
  $b_1=41/10$ ($8b_1=(4/5)\cdot41$, \vref{v434}); and the discriminant exponent
  $-\tfrac54=-q(D_5)$ (\vref{v341}). The derived stationarity must then have
  $\alpha^{-1}=137.0359992168$ as its unique physical root (\vref{v3}).
\end{itemize}
The open content splits into two named faces (one target, per \vref{v434}/\vref{v435}): the
\emph{analytic} face --- T1 plus the identification of $\delta\log\det_\zeta$ with the seam
Seeley--DeWitt data, i.e.\ the diagonal $\zeta$-renormalisation at the marks and the
multiplicity matching $41=10\,b_1$ (\vref{v484}/\vref{v485}) --- and the \emph{topological}
face, the \texttt{EM1} lemma: the from-first-principles proof that
$\partial_\tau\log Z_{\mathrm{Maxwell}}=k_0\,\alpha^3$ with $k_0$ the bulk Chern level
(\vref{v48}/\vref{v435}).
\emph{Both named faces now carry computed witnesses (2026-08-27,
\texttt{ALPHA.QUILLEN.FACES.01} \mE\ new; \veri{v974\_alpha\_faces\_computed.py};
\texttt{ALPHA.QUILLEN.EXACT.01} stays \mO):} the diagonal-$\zeta$ (analytic) face is executed
as a BFK gluing --- the gluing constant is exactly $2^{-N}$, the jump matrix
$16\,\cthree\,\operatorname{circ}(2,-1,0,-1)$ \emph{is} the inverse mark Green matrix, and the
\vref{v485} scale channel is reproduced via the KMS unit, while against the \vref{v484} mark
matrix the comparison is channel-wise exact but with the kernels in \emph{swapped} $\mu_4$
channels, so the analytic face is \emph{sharpened} to the channel-swap question; the
topological (\texttt{EM1}) face is computed --- pumped charge $=$ spectral flow $=$
St\v{r}eda $=$ Chern $=1$ on the \vref{v367} collar; and the finite Quillen identity is
verified \emph{two-sided} (Berry integral $=$ Poincar\'e--Lelong divisor integral $=+1$, the
difference an exact form). The remaining open content is typed: the rigidity premise, the
channel-swap question, and the continuum Bismut--Freed identification;
$\alpha^{-1}=137.0359992168$ is re-anchored with split residual $6.4\times10^{-58}$.
\emph{Channel-swap update (2026-08-28, external master-object review wave 2;
\veri{v985\_quillen\_channel\_swap.py}; the contract stays \mO):} the swap question is
\emph{resolved at the finite level} by $\mu_4$-character grading.  Exactly: the jump pattern
$\operatorname{circ}(2,-1,0,-1)$ has $\mu_4$ eigenvalues $[0,2,4,2]$ (kernel in the $k{=}0$
channel), the mark pattern $\operatorname{circ}(0,1,2,1)$ has $[4,-2,0,-2]$ (kernel in
$k{=}2$); the deck swap $r\to r{+}2$ ($P_0\leftrightarrow P_2$, $P_1\leftrightarrow P_3$) maps
the jump $|$spectrum$|$ onto the mark pattern \emph{exactly} (the odd swap $r\to r{+}1$ fails
--- must-fail fires), so after the swap both kernels sit in the same channel and the graded
determinant $\log\det'_{\mathrm{gr}}=\sum_r(-1)^r\log\det'(\Delta|_{P_r})$ compares them well-definedly:
the graded difference is $\cthree$-\emph{free}, $\log(\ln 2/4)$ exactly, and the channelwise
unit ratio is uniform $=4/\ln 2$ in every matched nonzero channel (the \vref{v974} doublet
ratio extended to full uniformity).  \emph{Completeness-wave notes (2026-08-28):} the finite
KMS rigidity premise is now executed
(\veri{v995\_kms\_subnet\_rigidity.py}: all $33$ mixing directions killed; covariance redundant;
$196608\to 0$); type-III/continuum stays \mO.  The finite-collar detline curvature converges
exponentially to $2\pi$ with rate $\sim 0.6936\sim\ln 2$
(\veri{v996\_gauge\_grammar\_kill.py}) --- a numerical Bismut--Freed shadow, not the continuum
theorem.  Still \mO: the continuum Bismut--Freed identification, type-III KMS uniqueness,
and the $2^{-4}$/Quillen-metric compensation.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Executed update (2026-08-30, late-evening harvest;
\veri{v1014\_\allowbreak bridge\_\allowbreak refinements.py}; the contract stays \mO).}
Finite restriction isomorphism verified; orientation fixes the conjugate branch
($2\to1$); the residual constant phase is fixed by the $A_0$ reference normalization ---
recorded.  Continuum Bismut--Freed stays \mO.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(5) Must-fail controls.} A submitted derivation is accepted only
if it respects the following (each realised in the suite):
\begin{itemize}
\item \textbf{MF-A1 (no experimental $\alpha$).} The functional and its stationarity must be
  constructed from $(\cthree, b_1, k_0, \varphi_{\mathrm{seam}})$ alone; any appearance of the
  measured $\alpha$ (CODATA or otherwise) at \emph{any} stage of the construction invalidates
  the derivation --- the measured value is compared \emph{after}, never inserted
  (\vref{v3}).
\item \textbf{MF-A2 (ablations destroy the hit).} Ablating the deck/carrier structure ---
  neighbouring integer budgets for $41$, dropping the $\mu_4$ factor, or a Chern level
  $k_0\neq1$ --- must move the root away from $137.0359992$ beyond the corpus tolerance
  (the \vref{v3} ablation battery; \vref{v48}).
\item \textbf{MF-A3 (trivial/reversed collar).} The trivial collar $M=3$ must give $C=0$ (no
  level, no fixed point of this form); the orientation flip $M=-1$ must give $C=-1$
  (\vref{v470}/\vref{v472}).
\item \textbf{MF-A4 (the metric-independence partition).} Any proposed identification must
  reproduce the $\pi$-power ladder $\{0,3,6\}$: a derivation that attaches a
  $(4\pi)^{-d/2}$ heat-kernel measure to the cubic term (or strips it from the quadratic or
  transport terms) fails (\vref{v435}).
\end{itemize}

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{(6) Acceptance tests} (the external review's three, verbatim, plus
one structural):

\item \textbf{A1 --- no experimental $\alpha$ input:} the derivation is checked to contain no
  use of the measured value anywhere; $\alpha^{-1}$ emerges as output only.
\item \textbf{A2 --- unique physical fixed point:} the \emph{derived} variational equation has
  exactly one physical root, and it equals $137.0359992168\ldots$ to the corpus precision.
\item \textbf{A3 --- ablations destroy the hit:} MF-A2/MF-A3 verified to fail, machine-checkably.
\item \textbf{A4 --- structural:} T1 is accepted only with the uniform-gap hypothesis explicit
  (the Dai--Freed section nonvanishing over the whole moduli torus); T2 modulo T1 (or vice
  versa) narrows the target and is recorded as progress, but only the pair closes it. Any
  marker move happens in \texttt{status\_ledger.csv} by the house process, never in this
  document.
\end{itemize}

\section*{Certifiability and recommended order}
\addcontentsline{toc}{section}{Certifiability and order}
% =====================================================================
\begin{center}\footnotesize
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.2}
\begin{tabularx}{\linewidth}{@{}Ylc@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Building block} & \textbf{Machine} & \textbf{Hardness} \\
\midrule
$W_{\mathrm{wall}}$ enumeration ($C_U^{(1)}$) & Lean / Python / Wolfram & easy (\emph{done}) \\
$D_4$ fixed-locus polynomial ($C_U^{(2)}$) & Sage / Mathematica & medium \\
selector uniqueness ($C_U^{(3)}$) & interval arithmetic & medium \\
SNF, determinants, spectra & Lean & easy--medium \\
principal-symbol ellipticity (G1) & xAct / Cadabra / Lean & medium \\
gap inequality (G5) & Lean & easy (\emph{done}) \\
operator-norm bound (G5) & analysis $+$ numerics & hard \\
projective limit (G6) & not primarily machine & very hard \\
Ward identities (G7) & BV/BRST formal $+$ checks & hard \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabularx}
\end{center}

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{Recommended order}.
\[
  \boxed{\ \text{Selector-triangle pairings}\ \rightarrow\ v_{\mathrm{geo}}\ \text{scale anchor}\ \rightarrow\ G_{\mathrm{net}}\ \text{inclusion theorem}\ }
\]
The flavor interface (historically $U_{\mathrm{wall}}$) is now reduced to the \emph{selector-triangle
pairings} (the dual normal pair $(d,n)$ forcing $R$ columnwise, \vref{v136}/\vref{v139}); it is finite,
falsifiable and the cheapest visible upgrade. The $v_{\mathrm{geo}}$ scale anchor is the one dimensionful
input (same nature as $1/G$). $G_{\mathrm{net}}$ --- the index-4 seam-net inclusion certifying the metric
sector --- is more fundamental but a \emph{programme}, not a single proof: heavy machinery, with the
projective limit the genuine summit. $F_{\mathrm{transfer}}$ (source$\to$pole / relic / cosmology) is the
remaining downstream interface.

\par\medskip\noindent\textbf{$F_{\mathrm{transfer}}$ is a typed functor, not a bag of open topics.}
It is standard physics fed TFPT \emph{source} data, $F_{\mathrm{transfer}}=F_{\mathrm{observable}}\circ
F_{\mathrm{threshold}}\circ F_{\mathrm{RG}}$, with exactly four interfaces:
\begin{center}\footnotesize
\renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.25}
\begin{tabularx}{\linewidth}{@{}llcX@{}}
\toprule
\textbf{Interface} & \textbf{Map} & \textbf{Status} & \textbf{Source data $\to$ observable} \\
\midrule
$F_{\mathrm{pole}}$ & source$\to$pole masses & \mE\,/\,\mC & Koide $53/54 = a^T(R{+}Q)\mathbf1/(2\,\mathbf1^TRa)$ is an exact operator factor \mE\ (the missing Sheet-Diamond corner); the pole-transfer interpretation is \mC\ (\veri{v183\_koide\_f\_corner\_transfer.py}) \\
$F_{\mathrm{Boltzmann}}$ & CP source$\to\eta_B$ & \mC & $\tilde m_1{=}m_3/A_\Lambda$ anchors the washout, but $M_1{=}M_R\varphi_0^4$ only relocates the free input ($M_R$ is the seesaw scale, not a compiler power), so $\eta_B$ stays a \emph{sharper scenario}, not a closure (\veri{v184\_etaB\_anchored\_boltzmann.py}) \\
$F_{\mathrm{relic}}$ & $(f_a,m_a,\theta_i,N_{\mathrm{DW}})\to\Omega_a$ & \mC & the determinant-line axion can be all of DM, but only on the $\theta_i\!\approx\!170^\circ$ hilltop where the relic is exponentially sensitive --- a viable scenario (\veri{v185\_axion\_relic\_solver.py}) \\
$F_{\mathrm{QCD}}$ & $(\alpha_s,b_3,\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}},\text{lattice})\to m_p/m_e$ & \mO & a QCD matching contract ($b_3{=}-7$ a carrier output), \emph{not} a compiler number --- the rejected $1836$ near-formula is the discipline working \\
\bottomrule
\end{tabularx}
\end{center}
\noindent A machine guard enforces the typing: the four families must always carry a \mC/\mO\ marker and
must never be promoted to a primitive \mE\ compiler prediction (\veri{v187\_ftransfer\_laws.py}). Exact
\emph{algebraic} sub-parts (the Koide factor, $b_3{=}-7$) may be \mE; the \emph{physical} prediction never
is.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The functor contract \texttt{CONTRACT.F.01}, four axioms
(\veri{v213\_ftransfer\_functor.py}).} Beyond the typing guard, the functor itself satisfies four checkable
structural axioms, each instance a \emph{discrete} compiler kernel \mE\ composed with an \emph{external}
continuous solver \mC: \textbf{(1) $\mu_4$-deck equivariance} --- the discrete kernels are carrier/deck
invariants $\{|\mu_4|,\Nfam,\gcar,A_\Lambda\}$, and the Koide multiplier $\lambda_2{=}(2/3)^6{=}64/729$
\emph{is} the $\mu_4$-deck transfer eigenvalue (\vref{v82}/\vref{v54}); \textbf{(2) Pl\"ucker preservation}
--- the dimensionless readouts are Pl\"ucker readings ($53{=}a^T(R{+}Q)\mathbf1$, $\mathrm{Pl}(F){=}(1,22,30)$;
$\|\mathrm{Pl}(K)\|_1{=}11$); \textbf{(3) positivity/stochasticity} --- $\operatorname{spec}T{=}\{1,(2/3)^6,(1/3)^6\}$
is positive (RP/OS), $\kappa_f{\in}(0,1)$; \textbf{(4) external modules explicit} --- the continuous content
(pole transfer, the flavored Boltzmann network, the finite-$T$ $\chi(T)$ ODE, the lattice $C_N$) is named and
external, never a compiler number. So \texttt{CONTRACT.F.01} is the third research contract alongside
$(U_{\mathrm{wall}})$ and $(G_{\mathrm{metric}})$: a typed functor, \emph{not} a bag of open topics --- but a
consolidation, \emph{not} a closure.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{A preregistered cross-ratio check for the four solvers ---
designed, and since executed on its discrete half.}
The design is machine-checked in \veri{v566\_parabolic\_anchor\_selfcode.py}, the discrete-half
execution in \veri{v571\_cr\_discrete\_corners.py}.  The disc~$-7$ norm
line $\alpha_{t+1}=\alpha_t+2$ (\vref{v533}) equips the transfer path with a projective invariant:
$\operatorname{CR}(\alpha_{-2},\alpha_{-1};\alpha_0,\alpha_1)=\tfrac43$ \emph{exactly} --- and,
honesty first, $\tfrac43$ is the \emph{universal} cross-ratio of any arithmetic progression, so the
test content is not the number but the \emph{discipline}: if the four solvers $(J,K,C,F)$ export
canonical one-dimensional projective states $y_J,y_K,y_C,y_F$ --- declared \emph{before} any
evaluation --- then M\"obius transport along the path forces
$\operatorname{CR}(y_J,y_K;y_C,y_F)=\tfrac43$, and the fourth state is determined fit-free by the
first three ($y_F$ reconstruction identity).  Negative control with teeth: the raw determinant path
$(2,4,14,32)$ gives $\operatorname{CR}=\tfrac{28}{25}\neq\tfrac43$ --- the already-known norm
sequence cannot pass.  \emph{The discrete half is now executed}: the four corners are the in-repo
kernels $J,K,C,F=M(1,t)=R+Q\,\mathrm{diag}(1,t,t)$, $t=-2..1$ (\vref{v224}), and the corpus's one
canonical declared solver reading --- the Koide anchor response $\ell(M)=a^{\mathsf T}M\mathbf 1$
(\vref{v183}) --- reads $(26,35,44,53)$ on them: $\operatorname{CR}=\tfrac43$ exactly, and the
fit-free reconstruction \emph{forces} $y_F=53$ from $(26,35,44)$ alone --- the Koide numerator is
M\"obius-determined by the three upstream corners (a new exact cross-corner identity); honesty
theorem stated out loud: $\ell$ is affine on the path (step $9=\Nfam^2$), and \emph{every} linear
functional of the kernel passes --- the check separates linear from nonlinear exports (the
dominant-eigenvalue export fails at $1.3222$, the determinants at $\tfrac{28}{25}$, both as they
must) \mE.  Status: the \emph{continuous} half (the external solvers exporting their own states)
stays open exactly as this contract fences it; contract markers unchanged \mC.  \emph{The frozen
export protocol for the continuous half} (design \mX, preregistered here): each instance must
export, \emph{before} evaluation, the canonical one-dimensional datum of its own native flow ---
the fixed-point multiplier of its contraction (cf.\ the native rates of \vref{v425}: F\_pole
$(2/3)^6$ exact in-corpus, F\_Boltzmann/F\_relic/F\_QCD external) --- as a projective state on
the path; only then is $\operatorname{CR}=\tfrac43$ tested, with no renormalisation after the
fact.  \emph{Executed} (\veri{v575\_cr\_continuous\_uniflow.py}): the in-repo native exports
form the quadruple $\{(2/3)^6,(2/3)^6,1,-7\}$ --- the repetition is \emph{forced} by the
single-flow theorem (\vref{v425}: F\_pole and F\_Boltzmann are literally the same seam
contraction) --- and a repeated quadruple gives $\operatorname{CR}\in\{0,1,\infty\}$ in every
corner assignment (theorem, plus all $24$ permutations): the continuous half can never pass
\emph{natively}; it closes onto the single-flow reduction, verdict \textsc{uniflow-degenerate}.
The four independent external data are the dimensionful anchors ($v_{\mathrm{geo}}$, $M_R$,
$C_p$, $\theta_i$) --- \mO\ walls, not projective states --- so a future continuous test needs
genuinely \emph{external} anchor-level exports; the meaningful cross-ratio content was and
remains the discrete half.  Both halves of the check are now executed \mE/\mC.
\emph{The jet-level successor is executed natively too}
(\veri{v578\_native\_jets.py}): when point values degenerate, the M\"obius-invariant
\emph{Schwarzian} of the exported trajectories is the right jet test --- and natively, the
exact v99 Koide trajectory has $\{q,t\}=-\Delta^2/2$ in wall time (constant,
trajectory-independent; F\_Boltzmann identical by the single-flow theorem) while being
M\"obius in its \emph{own} exponential clock $u=e^{\Delta t}$ (Schwarzian $0$), and the
$1$-loop QCD flow is M\"obius in its own RG time already (Schwarzian $0$): the native
quadruple $\{-\Delta^2/2,\,-\Delta^2/2,\,0,\,\text{degenerate}\}$ shares \emph{no}
wall-time jet invariant --- each instance is \emph{projective in its own clock}, the
obstruction is the clock, and the clocks are anchored by the external data.  Verdict
\textsc{moebius-in-own-clock}; a future joint jet test needs external anchor-level jets;
markers unchanged \mE/\mC.
\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The discrete$\to$dynamic lens: \texttt{F\_transfer} is the readout end of the one
principle (\veri{v303\_ftransfer\_dynamics.py}, \texttt{FR.DYNAMICS.01}).} The typing above is sharpened by a
\emph{dynamical} reading. The main-branch mechanism is a gapped, positivity-preserving relaxation to a
\emph{unique} attractor (the local-averaging update $m\!\leftarrow\!(A{+}2I)/4\,m\to$ the $E_8$ marks, gap
$6\ln(3/2)$). All four \texttt{F\_transfer} instances share that \emph{same shape}: \mE\ \textbf{F\_pole} (Koide)
\emph{is} the seam dynamics --- the source$\to$pole/branch relaxation is the M\"obius map with fixed-point
multiplier $F'{=}(2/3)^6{=}\lambda_2$, the seam-clock subleading eigenvalue, so $|F'|<1\Rightarrow$ a unique
attractor at the \emph{seam} rate (\vref{v82}); \mC\ \textbf{F\_Boltzmann} ($\eta_B$) is a gapped positive
contraction (washout $\kappa_f\in(0,1)$ toward the unique balance, an H-theorem); \mC\ \textbf{F\_relic} (axion)
freezes at an adiabatic fixed point (the comoving number relaxes to a constant); \mE\ \textbf{F\_QCD} ($m_p/m_e$)
is the 1-loop RG flow with the carrier $b_3{=}-7$, whose Gaussian UV fixed point is the attractor (asymptotic
freedom, $\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}}$ generated). \emph{Honest boundary:} only F\_pole has the seam rate $(2/3)^6$
exactly; the other three share the \emph{shape} with external rates (thermal, cosmological, RG). So
\texttt{F\_transfer} is not a bolt-on but the downstream manifestation of the one discrete$\to$dynamic principle
--- the compiler fixes the discrete seed $+$ integer kernel, a gapped flow carries it to the observable --- and
the theory's simplicity is preserved precisely because the external rates are honestly fenced (\vref{v187}), not
because they reduce to the seam. (Even F\_pole's flow time is non-integer, \vref{v93}: the relaxation is the
\emph{continuous} semigroup $e^{t\log F}$ generated by the discrete operator --- exactly the diffusion-semigroup
reading of the main branch.)

$\dot q{=}(\Delta/\Nfam)(q{-}2)(q{-}5)$ linearised at the attractor $q{=}2$ has rate $-\Delta$, so the

pole scale and $M_R$ are $v_{\mathrm{geo}}$-class, $C_p$ is the lattice $O(1)$, and $F_{\mathrm{relic}}$'s
cosmological initial condition $\theta_i$ is the one genuine wall. A reduction, \emph{not} internalisation:
no new premise, and the firewall (\vref{v187}) holds --- the observable stays \mC.

\par\smallskip\noindent\textbf{The frozen external-clock contract
(\texttt{FTRANSFER.CLOCKS.01}) --- preregistered 2026-08-04,
executed 2026-08-05.}
Since both thermal-time routes are dead, the clocks \emph{must} be
external --- and without clocks fixed in advance every jet statement
is arbitrarily reparametrizable.  The contract therefore freezes
them (design byte-frozen 2026-08-04 in
\texttt{experiments/ftransfer-clocks/}\allowbreak
\texttt{hypotheses/ftransfer\_clocks\_v1.yaml};
typed \mX, a search-target design, \emph{not} a claim).  \emph{The
four frozen clocks, exactly one physical clock per solver:}
F\_pole $=$ proper time $\tau$ (the on-shell/pole-mass phase clock;
frozen gauge: one \vref{v99} seam unit $=$ one proper-phase e-fold);
F\_Boltzmann $=$ inverse temperature $\beta$ (FIRAS-anchored
adiabatic chart; one washout seam unit $=$ one $\beta$ e-fold; the
$z$-linear alternative is recorded as \emph{excluded});
F\_QCD $=$ RG time $t=\ln(\mu/M_Z)$ ($\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$,
$1$-loop $b_0=7$ on the
frozen window $[m_t,1\,\mathrm{TeV}]$; identity gauge);
F\_relic $=$ cosmological e-folds $\ln a$ (Planck 2018 background;
the flow is frozen, the jets \emph{degenerate} --- the channel is
scored only on kills and dictionary validity).  \emph{The
preregistered prediction} (FTC.P1): under the frozen affine
dictionaries (slopes $-1,-1,+1,-1$ into the common chart
$\sigma=\ln(\mu/M_Z)$) \emph{one} constant-coefficient Riccati
equation carries all non-degenerate transported jets ---
equivalently, the transported Schwarzians of
F\_pole/F\_Boltzmann/F\_QCD are \emph{equal}; the seam channels
transport to $-\Delta^2/2=-2.959235170076978$ exactly, so the
prediction forces the transported QCD Schwarzian onto that value.
\emph{Three kills:} K1 \textsc{no-common-connection} (symbolic
inequality of the transported Schwarzians; on kill the executor must
exhibit the forbidden reparametrization per failing pair); K2
\textsc{free-reparam-needed} (any post-freeze change of a gauge or
dictionary; the YAML sha256 is recorded by the executor); K3
\textsc{second-clock-needed} (any channel export needing two time
variables).  \emph{The honest prior, preregistered:} the
\emph{expected} outcome is the K1 kill --- affine transport scales a
Schwarzian by $\mathrm{slope}^2$ and cannot move $0$ (the QCD value)
onto $-\Delta^2/2$ (the seam value); a K1 kill would finalize the
\vref{v578} diagnosis at the \emph{physical}-clock level and close
the jet route of \texttt{CONTRACT.F.01}; \textsc{one-class} would be
the surprise.  \emph{Executed (2026-08-05,
\veri{v777\_ftransfer\_clock\_jets.py}, one module from two probes,
$24+14$ checks, $0$ failures):} the K1 kill fired \emph{exactly} as
the frozen prior expected --- verdict \textsc{no-common-connection}.
